`README.md`:

```md
# Crosshair Z

Lightweight custom crosshair overlay for Windows games.

## Lightweight

- **~100 MB RAM while gaming**: closing the settings window frees it completely; only the tiny overlay keeps running
- **Near-zero CPU**: the overlay only redraws when something changes; mouse buttons are polled only if a feature needs them (right-click mode or firing animation)
- **Offline-first**: no accounts, telemetry or ads. The only network use is an optional update check on startup (one small request to GitHub, can be turned off in Settings); the app works the same offline
- **Anti-cheat friendly**: a plain transparent window on top of the game, no DLL injection, no hooks into the game
- **One dependency** (`koffi`), portable exe, no installer, no background services

## Features

- Transparent, click-through overlay that stays on top of borderless/windowed games
- Layered designer on a pixel grid: lines, outline, center dot, circle, image layers, blur
- Firing animation (spread / kick) on left mouse button
- Toggle by hotkey or right mouse button (hide or switch to a second crosshair)
- Per-crosshair hotkeys, monitor selection, pixel offset, overall size
- Share codes (`CZ2-…`)
- Import of your own saved Crosshair X crosshairs from local data files
- Newer version launched while an older one runs → automatic handover
- Optional update check: one-click update downloads the new portable exe next to the old one and hands over

## Development

```bash
npm install
npm start          # run app
npm test           # unit tests
npm run dist       # portable exe → release/
```

Dev aids:
- `CZ_CAPTURE_DIR=<dir>` renders every page hidden and saves PNGs, then quits
- `CZ_USERDATA=<dir>` runs with an isolated config

## Notes

- Exclusive fullscreen games hide overlays; use borderless/windowed mode.
- Crosshair X is a separate product. Crosshair Z only reads crosshair data you saved locally; it contains no Crosshair X code or assets.

```

`scripts/build-default-pack.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/*
 * Builds src/shared/default-pack.json from the author's saved Crosshair X crosshairs
 * (%APPDATA%\CrosshairX\crosshair-store). Workshop downloads are excluded.
 * Identical designs are kept once. Names are neutral descriptions ("White cross 2"),
 * original names are not shipped. Stored as compact CZ2 share codes.
 *
 * Usage: node scripts/build-default-pack.js [crosshairXDataDir]
 */
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const core = require('../src/shared/crosshair-core');
const { scanCrosshairX } = require('../src/main/crosshairx-scan');
const { describe } = require('../src/shared/crosshairx-import');

const OUT = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'shared', 'default-pack.json');
const log = { info: () => {}, warn: (m, c) => console.warn(m, c) };

function main() {
  const dataDir = process.argv[2];
  const res = scanCrosshairX(log, dataDir || undefined);
  if (!res.exists) throw new Error(`Crosshair X data dir not found: ${res.dataDir}`);
  const seen = new Set();
  const pack = [];
  const nameCount = new Map();
  for (const it of res.items) {
    if (it.source !== 'Saved') continue;
    if (core.hasImageLayers(it.crosshair)) continue;
    const fp = JSON.stringify(it.crosshair);
    if (seen.has(fp)) continue;
    seen.add(fp);
    const base = describe(it.crosshair);
    const n = (nameCount.get(base) || 0) + 1;
    nameCount.set(base, n);
    pack.push({ name: n === 1 ? base : `${base} ${n}`, code: core.encodeShareCode(it.crosshair) });
  }
  if (pack.length === 0) throw new Error('no crosshairs found to pack');
  fs.writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(pack, null, 1) + '\n', 'utf8');
  console.log(`wrote ${pack.length} crosshairs (from ${res.items.length} found, ${res.errors.length} unreadable) → ${OUT}`);
}

try {
  main();
} catch (err) {
  console.error('build-default-pack failed:', err.message);
  process.exit(1);
}

```

`src/main/crosshairx-scan.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { convertCrosshairX } = require('../shared/crosshairx-import');

const MAX_FILE_BYTES = 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_FILES = 500;

/** Default Crosshair X data dir (Windows roaming app data). */
function defaultDataDir() {
  return path.join(process.env.APPDATA || '', 'CrosshairX');
}

function listJson(dir) {
  try {
    return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })
      .filter((e) => e.isFile() && e.name.endsWith('.json') && e.name !== 'manifest.json')
      .map((e) => path.join(dir, e.name));
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code !== 'ENOENT') throw err;
    return [];
  }
}

function listDirs(dir) {
  try {
    return fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).filter((e) => e.isDirectory()).map((e) => path.join(dir, e.name));
  } catch (err) {
    if (err.code !== 'ENOENT') throw err;
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Read + convert one Crosshair X crosshair file.
 * @returns {{name:string, crosshair:object, warnings:string[]}}
 */
function convertFile(file) {
  const stat = fs.statSync(file);
  if (stat.size > MAX_FILE_BYTES) throw new Error('file too large');
  const text = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
  // No fallback name: unnamed crosshairs get a descriptive one ("Green cross").
  return convertCrosshairX(JSON.parse(text));
}

/**
 * Scan saved crosshairs + subscribed workshop crosshairs. Never throws; collects per-file errors.
 * @param {ReturnType<import('./logger').createLogger>} log
 * @param {string} [dataDir]
 */
function scanCrosshairX(log, dataDir = defaultDataDir()) {
  const sources = [{ label: 'Saved', files: listJson(path.join(dataDir, 'crosshair-store')) }];
  for (const mod of listDirs(path.join(dataDir, 'mods'))) {
    sources.push({ label: 'Workshop', files: listJson(path.join(mod, 'crosshairs')) });
  }
  const items = [];
  const errors = [];
  for (const src of sources) {
    for (const file of src.files) {
      if (items.length >= MAX_FILES) break;
      const rel = path.relative(dataDir, file);
      try {
        items.push({ id: rel, source: src.label, ...convertFile(file) });
      } catch (err) {
        errors.push({ file: rel, error: err.message });
        log.warn('crosshairx convert failed', { file: rel, error: err.message });
      }
    }
  }
  log.info('crosshairx scan', { dataDir, found: items.length, failed: errors.length });
  return { dataDir, exists: fs.existsSync(dataDir), items, errors };
}

module.exports = { scanCrosshairX, convertFile, defaultDataDir };

```

`src/main/logger.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const MAX_LOG_BYTES = 1024 * 1024;
const LEVELS = ['DEBUG', 'INFO', 'WARN', 'ERROR'];

/**
 * Create file+console logger with single-file rotation (.1 backup).
 * @param {string} dir log directory
 * @param {string} name file base name
 */
function createLogger(dir, name) {
  const file = path.join(dir, `${name}.log`);
  let fileOk = true;
  try {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  } catch (err) {
    fileOk = false;
    console.error('[logger] cannot create log dir', dir, err);
  }

  function rotateIfNeeded() {
    try {
      if (fs.statSync(file).size > MAX_LOG_BYTES) fs.renameSync(file, `${file}.1`);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code !== 'ENOENT') console.error('[logger] rotate failed', err);
    }
  }

  function write(level, msg, ctx) {
    const line = `${new Date().toISOString()} ${level} ${msg}${ctx ? ' ' + safeJson(ctx) : ''}`;
    (level === 'ERROR' ? console.error : console.log)(line);
    if (!fileOk) return;
    try {
      rotateIfNeeded();
      fs.appendFileSync(file, line + '\n', 'utf8');
    } catch (err) {
      fileOk = false;
      console.error('[logger] write failed, file logging disabled', err);
    }
  }

  const api = {};
  for (const lvl of LEVELS) api[lvl.toLowerCase()] = (msg, ctx) => write(lvl, msg, ctx);
  api.file = file;
  return api;
}

function safeJson(v) {
  if (v instanceof Error) return JSON.stringify({ message: v.message, stack: v.stack });
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  } catch {
    return String(v);
  }
}

module.exports = { createLogger };

```

`src/main/main.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const path = require('path');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { app, BrowserWindow, Tray, Menu, globalShortcut, ipcMain, screen, clipboard, nativeImage, dialog, net, shell } = require('electron');
const core = require('../shared/crosshair-core');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');
const { createMousePoller } = require('./mouse');
const { scanCrosshairX, convertFile } = require('./crosshairx-scan');
const { loadDefaultPack } = require('../shared/default-pack');
const { checkForUpdate, downloadUpdate, compareVersions } = require('./updater');
const { ConfigStore, cleanName, cleanAccel, newId, fingerprint, dedupeProfiles, DEFAULT_HOTKEYS, MAX_PROFILES } = require('./store');

const TOPMOST_REASSERT_MS = 2000;
const UPDATE_CHECK_DELAY_MS = 5000; // after boot, so startup is never slowed by the network
const UPDATE_HANDOVER_TIMEOUT_MS = 30000; // new version should have taken over by then
const MOVE_STEP_PX = 1;
const ICON_PATH = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'assets', 'icon.png');
const TRAY_ICON_PATH = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'assets', 'tray.png');
const RENDERER_DIR = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'renderer');
const PRELOAD = path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js');
const CAPTURE_PAGES = ['library', 'saved', 'import', 'share', 'designer', 'hotkeys', 'toggle', 'position', 'settings', 'designer:light', 'designer:game', 'designer:dark'];

// Capture mode must not share profile/cache dirs with a running instance.
if (process.env.CZ_CAPTURE_DIR) app.setPath('userData', path.join(process.env.CZ_CAPTURE_DIR, 'userdata'));
// Dev aid: CZ_USERDATA=<dir> runs normally with an isolated config (for live tests).
else if (process.env.CZ_USERDATA) app.setPath('userData', process.env.CZ_USERDATA);

const log = createLogger(path.join(app.getPath('userData'), 'logs'), 'crosshair-z');

/** Runtime state of the app (single owner: this module). */
const state = {
  store: null,
  overlay: null,
  settings: null,
  tray: null,
  mouse: null,
  topmostTimer: null,
  hotkeyStatus: {},
  hotkeysSuspended: false,
  quitting: false,
  capture: false,
  kbAlt: false, // keyboard toggle active (hide or switch)
  rcAlt: false, // right-click alternate active (hide or switch)
  firing: false,
  launchHidden: false, // 'hide crosshair on launch' until first toggle
  /** status: idle | checking | none | available | downloading | launched | error */
  update: { status: 'idle', release: null, error: null },
};

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => log.error('uncaughtException', err));
process.on('unhandledRejection', (err) => log.error('unhandledRejection', err instanceof Error ? err : { err }));

// ---------- config helpers ----------

function cfg() {
  return state.store.get();
}

function profileById(id) {
  return cfg().profiles.find((p) => p.id === id) || null;
}

function activeProfile() {
  return profileById(cfg().activeProfileId) || cfg().profiles[0];
}

/** Visible flag + crosshair after applying keyboard / right-click alternates. */
function effective() {
  const c = cfg();
  let visible = c.enabled && !state.launchHidden;
  let profile = activeProfile();
  if (state.kbAlt) {
    if (c.toggle.mode === 'hide') visible = false;
    else profile = profileById(c.toggle.profileId) || profile;
  }
  if (state.rcAlt) {
    if (c.rightClick.mode.startsWith('hide')) visible = false;
    else profile = profileById(c.rightClick.profileId) || profile;
  }
  return { visible, profile };
}

/** Apply mutation to a config copy, persist, push to all windows. */
function mutate(fn) {
  const next = structuredClone(cfg());
  fn(next);
  state.store.set(next);
  broadcast();
  return cfg();
}

function broadcast() {
  layoutOverlay();
  refreshTray();
  updateMousePolling();
  sendTo(state.settings, 'config:changed', cfg());
}

function sendTo(win, channel, data) {
  if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.webContents.send(channel, data);
}

// ---------- overlay ----------

function targetDisplay() {
  const id = cfg().display.monitorId;
  return screen.getAllDisplays().find((d) => d.id === id) || screen.getPrimaryDisplay();
}

function createOverlay() {
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 64,
    height: 64,
    show: false,
    frame: false,
    transparent: true,
    resizable: false,
    movable: false,
    focusable: false,
    skipTaskbar: true,
    hasShadow: false,
    alwaysOnTop: true,
    fullscreenable: false,
    backgroundColor: '#00000000',
    webPreferences: { preload: PRELOAD, contextIsolation: true, sandbox: true, nodeIntegration: false, backgroundThrottling: false },
  });
  win.setIgnoreMouseEvents(true);
  win.setAlwaysOnTop(true, 'screen-saver');
  win.setVisibleOnAllWorkspaces(true, { visibleOnFullScreen: true });
  win.webContents.on('render-process-gone', (_e, details) => {
    log.error('overlay renderer gone, recreating', details);
    recreateOverlay();
  });
  win.webContents.on('did-finish-load', () => layoutOverlay());
  win.loadFile(path.join(RENDERER_DIR, 'overlay.html')).catch((err) => log.error('overlay load failed', err));
  return win;
}

function recreateOverlay() {
  if (state.quitting) return;
  if (state.overlay && !state.overlay.isDestroyed()) state.overlay.destroy();
  state.overlay = createOverlay();
}

/** Size + position overlay so crosshair center = display center + offset (physical px). */
function layoutOverlay() {
  const win = state.overlay;
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
  const c = cfg();
  const { visible, profile } = effective();
  const ch = profile.crosshair;
  const display = targetDisplay();
  const sf = display.scaleFactor || 1;
  const sizePx = core.overlaySizePx(ch, c.display.scale);
  const sizeDip = Math.ceil(sizePx / sf);
  const centerX = display.bounds.x + display.bounds.width / 2 + c.display.offsetX / sf;
  const centerY = display.bounds.y + display.bounds.height / 2 + c.display.offsetY / sf;
  try {
    win.setBounds({
      x: Math.round(centerX - sizeDip / 2),
      y: Math.round(centerY - sizeDip / 2),
      width: sizeDip,
      height: sizeDip,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('overlay setBounds failed', err);
  }
  // Visibility is applied inside the renderer; the window itself stays shown. Hiding/showing a
  // transparent native window raced with its repaint and z-order, so toggles sometimes did nothing.
  sendTo(win, 'overlay:render', { crosshair: ch, scale: c.display.scale, firing: state.firing, visible });
  if (state.capture) return;
  if (!win.isVisible()) win.showInactive();
  win.setAlwaysOnTop(true, 'screen-saver');
}

/** Games grab z-order; reassert topmost periodically while visible. */
function startTopmostWatch() {
  state.topmostTimer = setInterval(() => {
    const win = state.overlay;
    if (!win || win.isDestroyed() || !win.isVisible()) return;
    try {
      win.setAlwaysOnTop(true, 'screen-saver');
      win.moveTop();
    } catch (err) {
      log.warn('topmost reassert failed', { error: err.message });
    }
  }, TOPMOST_REASSERT_MS);
}

// ---------- mouse (firing + right click) ----------

function needsMouse() {
  const c = cfg();
  if (c.rightClick.mode !== 'off') return true;
  const ids = [c.activeProfileId, c.toggle.profileId, c.rightClick.profileId];
  return ids.some((id) => {
    const p = profileById(id);
    return p && core.hasFiring(p.crosshair);
  });
}

function updateMousePolling() {
  if (!state.mouse || state.capture) return;
  if (needsMouse()) {
    state.mouse.start(onMouse);
  } else if (state.mouse.isRunning()) {
    state.mouse.stop();
    state.rcAlt = false;
    setFiring(false);
  }
}

function setFiring(on) {
  if (state.firing === on) return;
  state.firing = on;
  sendTo(state.overlay, 'overlay:fire', on);
}

function onMouse(now, prev) {
  if (now.left !== prev.left) setFiring(now.left);
  if (now.right === prev.right) return;
  const mode = cfg().rightClick.mode;
  if (mode === 'off') return;
  if (mode.endsWith('Hold')) state.rcAlt = now.right;
  else if (now.right) state.rcAlt = !state.rcAlt;
  else return;
  layoutOverlay();
}

// ---------- settings window ----------

function openSettings() {
  if (state.settings && !state.settings.isDestroyed()) {
    if (state.settings.isMinimized()) state.settings.restore();
    state.settings.show();
    state.settings.focus();
    return;
  }
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1240,
    height: 800,
    minWidth: 1000,
    minHeight: 640,
    title: 'Crosshair Z',
    icon: ICON_PATH,
    backgroundColor: '#191c22',
    autoHideMenuBar: true,
    // Custom title bar: page draws brand + controls, OS draws min/max/close on the right.
    titleBarStyle: 'hidden',
    titleBarOverlay: { color: '#20242b', symbolColor: '#95a0ae', height: 40 },
    show: false,
    webPreferences: { preload: PRELOAD, contextIsolation: true, sandbox: true, nodeIntegration: false },
  });
  win.setMenu(null);
  win.once('ready-to-show', () => win.show());
  // Destroy instead of hide: keeps the in-game footprint to the overlay only (~100 MB less).
  // The renderer flushes pending edits on beforeunload; the tray keeps the app alive.
  win.on('closed', () => {
    if (state.settings === win) state.settings = null;
    log.info('settings window closed');
  });
  win.webContents.on('render-process-gone', (_e, details) => log.error('settings renderer gone', details));
  win.webContents.on('console-message', (_e, level, message, line, source) => {
    if (level >= 2) log.warn('settings console', { message, source: `${source}:${line}` });
  });
  win.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(() => ({ action: 'deny' }));
  win.webContents.on('will-navigate', (e) => e.preventDefault());
  win.loadFile(path.join(RENDERER_DIR, 'settings.html')).catch((err) => log.error('settings load failed', err));
  state.settings = win;
}

// ---------- tray ----------

function loadIcon(file, size) {
  const img = nativeImage.createFromPath(file);
  if (img.isEmpty()) log.warn('icon missing', { file });
  return size && !img.isEmpty() ? img.resize({ width: size, height: size }) : img;
}

function createTray() {
  try {
    state.tray = new Tray(loadIcon(TRAY_ICON_PATH)); // tray.png + tray@2x.png picked by DPI
    state.tray.setToolTip('Crosshair Z');
    state.tray.on('click', () => openSettings());
    refreshTray();
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('tray creation failed', err);
  }
}

function refreshTray() {
  if (!state.tray) return;
  const c = cfg();
  const profileItems = c.profiles.slice(0, 30).map((p) => ({
    label: p.name,
    type: 'radio',
    checked: p.id === c.activeProfileId,
    click: () => equip(p.id),
  }));
  state.tray.setContextMenu(
    Menu.buildFromTemplate([
      ...(state.update.status === 'available'
        ? [{ label: `Update to ${state.update.release.version}`, click: () => installUpdate() }, { type: 'separator' }]
        : []),
      { label: 'Show crosshair', type: 'checkbox', checked: c.enabled, click: () => { state.launchHidden = false; state.kbAlt = false; mutate((n) => (n.enabled = !n.enabled)); } },
      { label: 'Crosshairs', submenu: profileItems },
      { type: 'separator' },
      { label: 'Open Crosshair Z', click: () => openSettings() },
      { label: 'Quit', click: () => app.quit() },
    ]),
  );
}

// ---------- actions ----------

function equip(id) {
  state.kbAlt = false;
  state.launchHidden = false;
  mutate((n) => {
    n.activeProfileId = id;
    n.enabled = true;
  });
}

/**
 * Show/hide hotkey. In 'hide' mode it drives the same `enabled` flag as the app's switch, so
 * switching off in the app and on via hotkey (or vice versa) always works. When the crosshair is
 * off, any mode's hotkey turns it back on. 'switch' mode otherwise swaps to the alternate profile.
 */
function keyboardToggle() {
  const c = cfg();
  if (state.launchHidden) {
    state.launchHidden = false;
    if (!c.enabled) mutate((n) => (n.enabled = true));
  } else if (c.toggle.mode === 'hide' || !c.enabled) {
    state.kbAlt = false;
    mutate((n) => (n.enabled = !c.enabled));
  } else {
    state.kbAlt = !state.kbAlt;
  }
  log.info('keyboard toggle', { enabled: cfg().enabled, alt: state.kbAlt, mode: c.toggle.mode });
  layoutOverlay();
  sendTo(state.settings, 'runtime:changed', runtime());
}

function cycleProfile(dir) {
  const c = cfg();
  const i = c.profiles.findIndex((p) => p.id === c.activeProfileId);
  equip(c.profiles[(i + dir + c.profiles.length) % c.profiles.length].id);
}

function moveCrosshair(dx, dy) {
  mutate((n) => {
    n.display.offsetX += dx * MOVE_STEP_PX;
    n.display.offsetY += dy * MOVE_STEP_PX;
  });
}

function runtime() {
  const u = state.update;
  return {
    kbAlt: state.kbAlt,
    launchHidden: state.launchHidden,
    mouseAvailable: state.mouse ? state.mouse.available : null,
    update: { status: u.status, version: u.release ? u.release.version : null, error: u.error, current: app.getVersion() },
  };
}

// ---------- updates (optional; the app never depends on them) ----------

function setUpdate(patch) {
  state.update = { ...state.update, ...patch };
  sendTo(state.settings, 'runtime:changed', runtime());
  refreshTray();
}

async function runUpdateCheck() {
  if (state.capture || ['checking', 'downloading', 'launched'].includes(state.update.status)) return;
  setUpdate({ status: 'checking', error: null });
  try {
    const release = await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: (url, opts) => net.fetch(url, opts), currentVersion: app.getVersion(), log });
    setUpdate({ status: release ? 'available' : 'none', release });
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('update check crashed', err);
    setUpdate({ status: 'error', error: err.message });
  }
}

/**
 * Download the new portable exe next to the running one and start it; the single-instance
 * handover then quits this version. Dev/unpacked builds just open the release page.
 */
async function installUpdate() {
  const { release } = state.update;
  if (!release || state.update.status === 'downloading') return;
  if (!process.env.PORTABLE_EXECUTABLE_FILE) {
    if (release.page) await shell.openExternal(release.page);
    return;
  }
  setUpdate({ status: 'downloading', error: null });
  try {
    const exe = await downloadUpdate({ fetchFn: (url, opts) => net.fetch(url, opts), release, dir: path.dirname(LAUNCH_EXE), log });
    const child = spawn(exe, [], { detached: true, stdio: 'ignore' });
    child.on('error', (err) => {
      log.error('update launch failed', { exe, error: err.message });
      setUpdate({ status: 'error', error: `Could not start the new version: ${err.message}` });
    });
    child.unref();
    setUpdate({ status: 'launched' });
    log.info('update launched, waiting for handover', { exe });
    // normally the handover quits this process long before; if not, tell the user where the exe is
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (state.update.status !== 'launched') return;
      log.warn('update handover timed out', { exe });
      setUpdate({ status: 'error', error: `Version ${release.version} was saved as "${exe}" but did not take over. Close this window from the tray (Quit) and start it.` });
    }, UPDATE_HANDOVER_TIMEOUT_MS);
  } catch (err) {
    setUpdate({ status: 'error', error: `Download failed: ${err.message}` });
  }
}

const HOTKEY_ACTIONS = {
  toggle: () => keyboardToggle(),
  next: () => cycleProfile(1),
  prev: () => cycleProfile(-1),
  settings: () => openSettings(),
  moveUp: () => moveCrosshair(0, -1),
  moveDown: () => moveCrosshair(0, 1),
  moveLeft: () => moveCrosshair(-1, 0),
  moveRight: () => moveCrosshair(1, 0),
};

/** Register one accelerator; returns status string. */
function tryRegister(accel, fn, used) {
  if (!accel) return 'empty';
  if (used.has(accel)) return 'duplicate';
  try {
    const ok = globalShortcut.register(accel, fn);
    if (ok) used.add(accel);
    return ok ? 'ok' : 'taken';
  } catch (err) {
    log.warn('hotkey invalid', { accel, error: err.message });
    return 'invalid';
  }
}

/** (Re)register global + per-crosshair hotkeys. Status keys: action names and `profile:<id>`. */
function registerHotkeys() {
  globalShortcut.unregisterAll();
  if (state.hotkeysSuspended) return state.hotkeyStatus;
  const status = {};
  const used = new Set();
  for (const [action, accel] of Object.entries(cfg().hotkeys)) status[action] = tryRegister(accel, HOTKEY_ACTIONS[action], used);
  for (const p of cfg().profiles) {
    if (p.hotkey) status[`profile:${p.id}`] = tryRegister(p.hotkey, () => equip(p.id), used);
  }
  state.hotkeyStatus = status;
  log.info('hotkeys registered', { count: used.size });
  sendTo(state.settings, 'hotkeys:status', status);
  return status;
}

/**
 * Register/unregister autostart. The portable build runs from a temp extraction dir that is
 * wiped on exit, so the login item must point at the original portable exe instead.
 */
function applyLoginItem() {
  const path = LAUNCH_EXE;
  const openAtLogin = cfg().launchAtLogin;
  try {
    app.setLoginItemSettings({ openAtLogin, path, args: ['--hidden'] });
    const now = app.getLoginItemSettings({ path, args: ['--hidden'] });
    log.info('login item applied', { openAtLogin, path, registered: now.openAtLogin, willLaunch: now.executableWillLaunchAtLogin });
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('setLoginItemSettings failed', { error: err.message, path });
  }
}

// ---------- IPC ----------

function requireString(v, name) {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || !v) throw new Error(`${name} must be non-empty string`);
  return v;
}

function handle(channel, fn) {
  ipcMain.handle(channel, async (_e, payload) => {
    try {
      return await fn(payload);
    } catch (err) {
      log.error(`ipc ${channel} failed`, { error: err.message });
      throw err;
    }
  });
}

const PATCH_FLAGS = ['enabled', 'launchAtLogin', 'startHidden', 'hideOnLaunch', 'checkUpdates'];

function applyPatch(n, p) {
  if (p.display && typeof p.display === 'object') Object.assign(n.display, p.display);
  if (p.toggle && typeof p.toggle === 'object') Object.assign(n.toggle, p.toggle);
  if (p.rightClick && typeof p.rightClick === 'object') Object.assign(n.rightClick, p.rightClick);
  for (const k of PATCH_FLAGS) if (typeof p[k] === 'boolean') n[k] = p[k];
}

function registerProfileIpc() {
  handle('profile:update', (p) => {
    const id = requireString(p && p.id, 'id');
    const out = mutate((n) => {
      const prof = n.profiles.find((x) => x.id === id);
      if (!prof) throw new Error(`profile ${id} not found`);
      if (p.crosshair) prof.crosshair = core.sanitizeCrosshair(p.crosshair);
      if (p.name !== undefined) prof.name = cleanName(p.name, prof.name);
      if (p.hotkey !== undefined) prof.hotkey = cleanAccel(p.hotkey);
    });
    if (p.hotkey !== undefined) registerHotkeys();
    return out;
  });

  handle('profile:create', (p) => {
    const activate = !p || p.activate !== false;
    // dedupe: an identical crosshair already saved → reuse it instead of adding a copy.
    if (p && p.dedupe) {
      const fp = fingerprint(p.crosshair);
      const existing = cfg().profiles.find((x) => fingerprint(x.crosshair) === fp);
      if (existing) {
        if (activate) equip(existing.id);
        return { id: existing.id, config: cfg(), existed: true };
      }
    }
    if (cfg().profiles.length >= MAX_PROFILES) throw new Error(`max ${MAX_PROFILES} crosshairs`);
    const id = newId();
    mutate((n) => {
      n.profiles.push({ id, name: cleanName(p && p.name, 'New Crosshair'), hotkey: '', crosshair: core.sanitizeCrosshair(p && p.crosshair) });
      if (!p || p.activate !== false) {
        n.activeProfileId = id;
        n.enabled = true;
      }
    });
    if (!p || p.activate !== false) state.kbAlt = false;
    return { id, config: cfg() };
  });

  handle('profile:delete', (id) => {
    requireString(id, 'id');
    if (cfg().profiles.length <= 1) throw new Error('cannot delete last crosshair');
    const out = mutate((n) => {
      n.profiles = n.profiles.filter((x) => x.id !== id);
      if (n.activeProfileId === id) n.activeProfileId = n.profiles[0].id;
    });
    registerHotkeys();
    return out;
  });

  handle('profile:importMany', (list) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(list) || list.length === 0) throw new Error('nothing to import');
    // Skip crosshairs already saved (and duplicates within the list itself).
    const seen = new Set(cfg().profiles.map((x) => fingerprint(x.crosshair)));
    const fresh = list.filter((it) => {
      const fp = fingerprint(it && it.crosshair);
      if (seen.has(fp)) return false;
      seen.add(fp);
      return true;
    });
    const duplicates = list.length - fresh.length;
    const free = MAX_PROFILES - cfg().profiles.length;
    const items = fresh.slice(0, Math.max(0, free));
    const overLimit = fresh.length - items.length;
    if (items.length === 0) return { config: cfg(), imported: 0, duplicates, overLimit };
    const ids = items.map(() => newId());
    const out = mutate((n) => {
      items.forEach((it, i) => {
        n.profiles.push({ id: ids[i], name: cleanName(it && it.name, 'Imported'), hotkey: '', crosshair: core.sanitizeCrosshair(it && it.crosshair) });
      });
      n.activeProfileId = ids[ids.length - 1];
      n.enabled = true;
    });
    log.info('imported crosshairs', { count: items.length, duplicates, overLimit });
    return { config: out, imported: items.length, duplicates, overLimit };
  });

  handle('profile:dedupe', () => {
    const c = cfg();
    const keepIds = new Set(dedupeProfiles(c.profiles, c.activeProfileId).map((p) => p.id));
    const removed = c.profiles.length - keepIds.size;
    if (removed === 0) return { config: c, removed: 0 };
    const out = mutate((n) => {
      n.profiles = n.profiles.filter((p) => keepIds.has(p.id));
    });
    registerHotkeys();
    log.info('removed duplicate crosshairs', { removed });
    return { config: out, removed };
  });

  handle('profile:activate', (id) => {
    equip(requireString(id, 'id'));
    return cfg();
  });

  handle('profile:move', (p) => {
    const id = requireString(p && p.id, 'id');
    return mutate((n) => {
      const i = n.profiles.findIndex((x) => x.id === id);
      const j = i + (p.dir > 0 ? 1 : -1);
      if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= n.profiles.length) return;
      [n.profiles[i], n.profiles[j]] = [n.profiles[j], n.profiles[i]];
    });
  });
}

function registerIpc() {
  const pack = loadDefaultPack();
  log.info('starter pack loaded', { count: pack.length });
  handle('config:get', () => ({ config: cfg(), hotkeyStatus: state.hotkeyStatus, presets: pack, runtime: runtime(), defaults: DEFAULT_HOTKEYS }));

  handle('config:patch', (p) => {
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') throw new Error('patch must be object');
    // switching on in the app must win over launch-hidden / hotkey-hidden state
    if (p.enabled === true) {
      state.launchHidden = false;
      if (cfg().toggle.mode === 'hide') state.kbAlt = false;
    }
    const out = mutate((n) => applyPatch(n, p));
    if (typeof p.launchAtLogin === 'boolean') applyLoginItem();
    if (p.checkUpdates === true && ['idle', 'error'].includes(state.update.status)) runUpdateCheck();
    if (p.toggle || p.rightClick) {
      state.kbAlt = false;
      state.rcAlt = false;
      layoutOverlay();
    }
    return out;
  });

  registerProfileIpc();

  handle('displays:list', () => {
    const primary = screen.getPrimaryDisplay().id;
    return screen.getAllDisplays().map((d, i) => ({
      id: d.id,
      label: `Display ${i + 1}${d.id === primary ? ' (primary)' : ''} — ${Math.round(d.size.width * d.scaleFactor)}×${Math.round(d.size.height * d.scaleFactor)} @ ${Math.round(d.scaleFactor * 100)}%`,
    }));
  });

  handle('hotkeys:set', (p) => {
    if (!p || typeof p !== 'object') throw new Error('hotkeys must be object');
    mutate((n) => {
      for (const k of Object.keys(n.hotkeys)) if (typeof p[k] === 'string') n.hotkeys[k] = p[k];
    });
    return registerHotkeys();
  });

  handle('hotkeys:suspend', (on) => {
    state.hotkeysSuspended = on === true;
    if (state.hotkeysSuspended) globalShortcut.unregisterAll();
    else registerHotkeys();
    return state.hotkeysSuspended;
  });

  handle('cx:scan', () => scanCrosshairX(log));

  handle('cx:openFile', async () => {
    const res = await dialog.showOpenDialog(state.settings, {
      title: 'Import Crosshair X crosshair',
      filters: [{ name: 'Crosshair X crosshair', extensions: ['json'] }],
      properties: ['openFile', 'multiSelections'],
    });
    if (res.canceled) return { items: [], errors: [] };
    const items = [];
    const errors = [];
    for (const file of res.filePaths.slice(0, 100)) {
      try {
        items.push({ id: file, source: 'File', ...convertFile(file) });
      } catch (err) {
        errors.push({ file: path.basename(file), error: err.message });
      }
    }
    return { items, errors };
  });

  handle('clipboard:write', (text) => clipboard.writeText(String(text).slice(0, 10000)));
  handle('clipboard:read', () => clipboard.readText().slice(0, 10000));
  handle('log:path', () => log.file);
  handle('update:check', () => runUpdateCheck());
  handle('update:install', () => installUpdate());
}

// ---------- lifecycle ----------

function onDisplaysChanged() {
  log.info('displays changed', { count: screen.getAllDisplays().length });
  layoutOverlay();
}

/** Dev aid: CZ_CAPTURE_DIR=<dir> renders windows hidden, saves PNG per page, quits. */
async function captureAndQuit(dir) {
  const fs = require('fs');
  const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
  try {
    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    await wait(2500);
    // hidden windows freeze CSS animations at their first frame; capture the settled state
    await state.settings.webContents.insertCSS('*, *::before, *::after { animation: none !important; transition: none !important; }');
    const shots = [['overlay', state.overlay, null], ...CAPTURE_PAGES.map((p) => [p, state.settings, p])];
    for (const [name, win, page] of shots) {
      if (page) {
        await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(`window.czShowPage(${JSON.stringify(page)})`);
        await wait(300);
      }
      const img = await win.webContents.capturePage(undefined, { stayHidden: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, `${name.replace(':', '-')}.png`), img.toPNG());
      log.info('captured', { name, size: img.getSize() });
    }
    // smallest allowed window: check that layouts still fit
    const [minW, minH] = state.settings.getMinimumSize();
    state.settings.setSize(minW, minH);
    await wait(400);
    for (const page of ['designer', 'library', 'saved']) {
      await state.settings.webContents.executeJavaScript(`window.czShowPage(${JSON.stringify(page)})`);
      await wait(300);
      const img = await state.settings.webContents.capturePage(undefined, { stayHidden: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, `${page}-min.png`), img.toPNG());
    }
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('capture failed', err);
  }
  app.quit();
}

function bootCapture(dir) {
  state.store = new ConfigStore(path.join(dir, 'userdata'), log);
  registerIpc();
  state.capture = true;
  state.overlay = createOverlay();
  openSettings();
  state.settings.removeAllListeners('ready-to-show');
  captureAndQuit(dir);
}

function boot() {
  if (process.env.CZ_CAPTURE_DIR) return bootCapture(process.env.CZ_CAPTURE_DIR);
  state.store = new ConfigStore(app.getPath('userData'), log);
  state.mouse = createMousePoller(log);
  state.launchHidden = cfg().hideOnLaunch;
  registerIpc();
  state.overlay = createOverlay();
  createTray();
  registerHotkeys();
  applyLoginItem();
  startTopmostWatch();
  updateMousePolling();
  screen.on('display-added', onDisplaysChanged);
  screen.on('display-removed', onDisplaysChanged);
  screen.on('display-metrics-changed', onDisplaysChanged);
  const hidden = process.argv.includes('--hidden') || cfg().startHidden;
  if (!hidden) openSettings();
  log.info('boot complete', { version: app.getVersion(), hidden });
  if (cfg().checkUpdates) setTimeout(runUpdateCheck, UPDATE_CHECK_DELAY_MS);
}


/** Launched exe path (portable builds run from a temp copy; env var holds the real file). */
const LAUNCH_EXE = process.env.PORTABLE_EXECUTABLE_FILE || process.execPath;

/**
 * Another instance was started. If it is a newer version, hand over: quit and relaunch that exe.
 * Otherwise just show our window.
 */
function onSecondInstance(_e, _argv, _cwd, data) {
  const incoming = data && typeof data.version === 'string' ? data.version : null;
  const exe = data && typeof data.exe === 'string' ? data.exe : null;
  if (incoming && exe && compareVersions(incoming, app.getVersion()) > 0) {
    log.info('newer version launched, handing over', { from: app.getVersion(), to: incoming, exe });
    app.relaunch({ execPath: exe, args: [] });
    app.quit();
    return;
  }
  openSettings();
}

const isolated = process.env.CZ_CAPTURE_DIR || process.env.CZ_USERDATA;
if (!isolated && !app.requestSingleInstanceLock({ version: app.getVersion(), exe: LAUNCH_EXE })) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  app.on('second-instance', onSecondInstance);
  app.whenReady().then(boot).catch((err) => {
    log.error('boot failed', err);
    app.exit(1);
  });
  // Tray app: keep running when all windows closed.
  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {});
  app.on('before-quit', () => {
    state.quitting = true;
    clearInterval(state.topmostTimer);
    if (state.mouse) state.mouse.stop();
    if (state.store) state.store.flush();
  });
  app.on('will-quit', () => globalShortcut.unregisterAll());
}

`src/main/mouse.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const POLL_MS = 8;
const VK_LBUTTON = 0x01;
const VK_RBUTTON = 0x02;
const KEY_DOWN_BIT = 0x8000;

/**
 * Global mouse button poller via user32 GetAsyncKeyState (works while games have focus).
 * Returns null-safe API; `available` false when FFI cannot load.
 * @param {ReturnType<import('./logger').createLogger>} log
 */
function createMousePoller(log) {
  let getKey = null;
  try {
    const koffi = require('koffi');
    const user32 = koffi.load('user32.dll');
    getKey = user32.func('short __stdcall GetAsyncKeyState(int vKey)');
    log.info('mouse poller ready');
  } catch (err) {
    log.error('mouse poller unavailable (firing/right-click features disabled)', { error: err.message });
  }

  let timer = null;
  let last = { left: false, right: false };

  function read() {
    try {
      return {
        left: (getKey(VK_LBUTTON) & KEY_DOWN_BIT) !== 0,
        right: (getKey(VK_RBUTTON) & KEY_DOWN_BIT) !== 0,
      };
    } catch (err) {
      log.error('GetAsyncKeyState failed, stopping poller', { error: err.message });
      stop();
      return last;
    }
  }

  /** Start polling; cb({left, right}, prev) fires only on change. Idempotent. */
  function start(cb) {
    if (!getKey || timer) return;
    last = read();
    timer = setInterval(() => {
      const now = read();
      if (now.left !== last.left || now.right !== last.right) {
        const prev = last;
        last = now;
        cb(now, prev);
      }
    }, POLL_MS);
    log.info('mouse polling started');
  }

  function stop() {
    if (!timer) return;
    clearInterval(timer);
    timer = null;
    last = { left: false, right: false };
    log.info('mouse polling stopped');
  }

  return { available: !!getKey, start, stop, isRunning: () => !!timer };
}

module.exports = { createMousePoller };

```

`src/main/preload.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');

const INVOKE_CHANNELS = new Set([
  'config:get',
  'config:patch',
  'profile:update',
  'profile:create',
  'profile:delete',
  'profile:activate',
  'profile:move',
  'profile:importMany',
  'profile:dedupe',
  'cx:scan',
  'cx:openFile',
  'displays:list',
  'hotkeys:set',
  'hotkeys:suspend',
  'clipboard:write',
  'clipboard:read',
  'log:path',
  'update:check',
  'update:install',
]);
const EVENT_CHANNELS = new Set(['config:changed', 'overlay:render', 'overlay:fire', 'hotkeys:status', 'runtime:changed']);

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('cz', {
  invoke(channel, payload) {
    if (!INVOKE_CHANNELS.has(channel)) return Promise.reject(new Error(`blocked channel ${channel}`));
    return ipcRenderer.invoke(channel, payload);
  },
  on(channel, cb) {
    if (!EVENT_CHANNELS.has(channel)) throw new Error(`blocked channel ${channel}`);
    const handler = (_e, data) => cb(data);
    ipcRenderer.on(channel, handler);
    return () => ipcRenderer.removeListener(channel, handler);
  },
});

`src/main/store.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const crypto = require('crypto');
const core = require('../shared/crosshair-core');
const { loadDefaultPack } = require('../shared/default-pack');

const CONFIG_VERSION = 5;
const SAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS = 300;
const MAX_PROFILES = 200;
const MAX_NAME_LEN = 40;
const MAX_OFFSET_PX = 4000;
const MAX_ACCEL_LEN = 64;
const TOGGLE_MODES = ['hide', 'switch'];
const RIGHT_CLICK_MODES = ['off', 'hideHold', 'hideToggle', 'switchHold', 'switchToggle'];

/** Only toggle is bound by default; the rest are optional (empty = not registered). */
const DEFAULT_HOTKEYS = Object.freeze({
  toggle: 'Alt+Shift+X',
  next: '',
  prev: '',
  settings: '',
  moveUp: '',
  moveDown: '',
  moveLeft: '',
  moveRight: '',
});

/** Toggle default before v4. */
const LEGACY_TOGGLE_DEFAULT = 'CommandOrControl+Shift+X';

/** Toggle default in v4. */
const V4_TOGGLE_DEFAULT = 'Alt+Shift+Z';

/** v1/v2 defaults for optional actions — cleared on migration to v3 unless user changed them. */
const LEGACY_DEFAULT_HOTKEYS = Object.freeze({
  next: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+Right',
  prev: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+Left',
  settings: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+S',
  moveUp: 'Alt+Shift+Up',
  moveDown: 'Alt+Shift+Down',
  moveLeft: 'Alt+Shift+Left',
  moveRight: 'Alt+Shift+Right',
});

function newId() {
  return crypto.randomUUID();
}

function cleanName(name, fallback) {
  const s = typeof name === 'string' ? name.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, MAX_NAME_LEN) : '';
  return s || fallback;
}

function clampNum(v, min, max, fallback) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return v !== null && v !== '' && Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.min(max, Math.max(min, n)) : fallback;
}

function clampInt(v, min, max, fallback) {
  return Math.round(clampNum(v, min, max, fallback));
}

function cleanAccel(v) {
  return typeof v === 'string' && v.length <= MAX_ACCEL_LEN ? v : '';
}

function obj(v) {
  return v && typeof v === 'object' ? v : {};
}

function defaultConfig() {
  // New users start with the first starter-pack crosshair (fallback: built-in preset).
  const first = loadDefaultPack()[0] || core.PRESETS[0];
  const profiles = [{ id: newId(), name: first.name, hotkey: '', crosshair: first.crosshair }];
  return {
    version: CONFIG_VERSION,
    enabled: true,
    activeProfileId: profiles[0].id,
    profiles,
    display: { monitorId: null, offsetX: 0, offsetY: 0, scale: 1 },
    hotkeys: { ...DEFAULT_HOTKEYS },
    toggle: { mode: 'hide', profileId: null },
    rightClick: { mode: 'off', profileId: null },
    launchAtLogin: false,
    startHidden: false,
    hideOnLaunch: false,
    checkUpdates: true,
  };
}

/**
 * Validate full config. Never throws; repairs instead.
 * @param {unknown} raw
 * @returns {object}
 */
function sanitizeConfig(raw) {
  const def = defaultConfig();
  const src = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {};
  const seen = new Set();
  const profiles = (Array.isArray(src.profiles) ? src.profiles : [])
    .slice(0, MAX_PROFILES)
    .filter((p) => p && typeof p === 'object')
    .map((p, i) => {
      let id = typeof p.id === 'string' && p.id.length <= 64 ? p.id : newId();
      if (seen.has(id)) id = newId();
      seen.add(id);
      return { id, name: cleanName(p.name, `Crosshair ${i + 1}`), hotkey: cleanAccel(p.hotkey), crosshair: core.sanitizeCrosshair(p.crosshair) };
    });
  if (profiles.length === 0) profiles.push(...def.profiles);

  const d = obj(src.display);
  const hk = obj(src.hotkeys);
  const version = Number(src.version) || 0;
  const hotkeys = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(DEFAULT_HOTKEYS)) {
    let v = typeof hk[k] === 'string' && hk[k].length <= MAX_ACCEL_LEN ? hk[k] : DEFAULT_HOTKEYS[k];
    if (version < 3 && v === LEGACY_DEFAULT_HOTKEYS[k]) v = '';
    // Toggle default moved Ctrl+Shift+X (<v4) → Alt+Shift+Z (v4) → Alt+Shift+X (v5).
    // Only untouched defaults migrate; a key the user picked themselves stays.
    if (version < 4 && k === 'toggle' && v === LEGACY_TOGGLE_DEFAULT) v = DEFAULT_HOTKEYS.toggle;
    if (version === 4 && k === 'toggle' && v === V4_TOGGLE_DEFAULT) v = DEFAULT_HOTKEYS.toggle;
    hotkeys[k] = v;
  }
  const ids = new Set(profiles.map((p) => p.id));
  const refId = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' && ids.has(v) ? v : null);
  const tg = obj(src.toggle);
  const rc = obj(src.rightClick);

  return {
    version: CONFIG_VERSION,
    enabled: typeof src.enabled === 'boolean' ? src.enabled : true,
    activeProfileId: profiles.some((p) => p.id === src.activeProfileId) ? src.activeProfileId : profiles[0].id,
    profiles,
    display: {
      monitorId: Number.isInteger(d.monitorId) ? d.monitorId : null,
      offsetX: clampInt(d.offsetX, -MAX_OFFSET_PX, MAX_OFFSET_PX, 0),
      offsetY: clampInt(d.offsetY, -MAX_OFFSET_PX, MAX_OFFSET_PX, 0),
      scale: Math.round(clampNum(d.scale, 0.25, 4, 1) * 100) / 100,
    },
    hotkeys,
    toggle: { mode: TOGGLE_MODES.includes(tg.mode) ? tg.mode : 'hide', profileId: refId(tg.profileId) },
    rightClick: { mode: RIGHT_CLICK_MODES.includes(rc.mode) ? rc.mode : 'off', profileId: refId(rc.profileId) },
    launchAtLogin: src.launchAtLogin === true,
    startHidden: src.startHidden === true,
    hideOnLaunch: src.hideOnLaunch === true,
    // optional update check: on unless the user turned it off
    checkUpdates: src.checkUpdates !== false,
  };
}

/** Canonical identity of a crosshair design (ignores name). */
function fingerprint(crosshair) {
  return JSON.stringify(core.sanitizeCrosshair(crosshair));
}

/**
 * Remove profiles whose crosshair is identical to another one. Keeps the active profile and
 * profiles with a hotkey in preference, otherwise the first occurrence; original order preserved.
 * @returns {object[]} kept profiles
 */
function dedupeProfiles(profiles, activeId) {
  const rank = (p) => (p.id === activeId ? 2 : 0) + (p.hotkey ? 1 : 0);
  const keep = new Set();
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const p of [...profiles].sort((a, b) => rank(b) - rank(a))) {
    const fp = fingerprint(p.crosshair);
    if (seen.has(fp)) continue;
    seen.add(fp);
    keep.add(p.id);
  }
  return profiles.filter((p) => keep.has(p.id));
}

/**
 * JSON config store with atomic writes and corrupt-file recovery.
 */
class ConfigStore {
  /**
   * @param {string} dir userData dir
   * @param {ReturnType<import('./logger').createLogger>} log
   */
  constructor(dir, log) {
    this.file = path.join(dir, 'config.json');
    this.log = log;
    this.timer = null;
    this.data = this.load();
  }

  load() {
    let text;
    try {
      text = fs.readFileSync(this.file, 'utf8').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.code === 'ENOENT') {
        this.log.info('config missing, using defaults', { file: this.file });
      } else {
        this.log.error('config read failed, using defaults', err);
      }
      return defaultConfig();
    }
    try {
      const cfg = sanitizeConfig(JSON.parse(text));
      this.log.info('config loaded', { profiles: cfg.profiles.length });
      return cfg;
    } catch (err) {
      const backup = `${this.file}.corrupt-${Date.now()}`;
      this.log.error('config corrupt, backing up and resetting', { backup, error: err.message });
      try {
        fs.copyFileSync(this.file, backup);
      } catch (copyErr) {
        this.log.error('config backup failed', copyErr);
      }
      return defaultConfig();
    }
  }

  get() {
    return this.data;
  }

  /** Replace config (sanitized) and schedule save. */
  set(next) {
    this.data = sanitizeConfig(next);
    this.scheduleSave();
    return this.data;
  }

  scheduleSave() {
    clearTimeout(this.timer);
    this.timer = setTimeout(() => this.flush(), SAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS);
  }

  /** Write synchronously via temp file + rename. */
  flush() {
    clearTimeout(this.timer);
    this.timer = null;
    const tmp = `${this.file}.tmp`;
    try {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(this.file), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(this.data, null, 2), 'utf8');
      fs.renameSync(tmp, this.file);
      this.log.debug('config saved');
    } catch (err) {
      this.log.error('config save failed', { file: this.file, error: err.message });
    }
  }
}

module.exports = { ConfigStore, sanitizeConfig, fingerprint, dedupeProfiles, defaultConfig, cleanName, cleanAccel, newId, DEFAULT_HOTKEYS, MAX_PROFILES };

`src/main/updater.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/*
 * Optional update check against GitHub releases. Never required for the app to work:
 * every failure is logged and reported as "no update"; nothing runs in the background.
 */

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const { Readable } = require('stream');
const { pipeline } = require('stream/promises');

const RELEASES_URL = 'https://api.github.com/repos/Nn1ff/crosshair-z/releases/latest';
const ASSET_NAME = 'Crosshair-Z.exe';
const DOWNLOAD_HOST_PREFIX = 'https://github.com/';
const CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS = 10000;
const CHECK_RETRIES = 2;
const RETRY_BASE_MS = 2000;
const DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;
const MAX_ASSET_BYTES = 300 * 1024 * 1024;
const VERSION_RE = /^v?(\d+(?:\.\d+){0,3})$/;

/** Compare dotted versions. Returns >0 when a is newer than b. */
function compareVersions(a, b) {
  const pa = String(a).split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const pb = String(b).split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(pa.length, pb.length); i++) {
    const d = (pa[i] || 0) - (pb[i] || 0);
    if (d) return d;
  }
  return 0;
}

/**
 * Extract the portable exe asset from a GitHub "latest release" payload.
 * @returns {{version: string, url: string, size: number, page: string} | null}
 */
function parseRelease(json) {
  if (!json || typeof json !== 'object' || json.draft || json.prerelease) return null;
  const m = typeof json.tag_name === 'string' ? VERSION_RE.exec(json.tag_name.trim()) : null;
  if (!m) return null;
  const asset = Array.isArray(json.assets) ? json.assets.find((a) => a && a.name === ASSET_NAME) : null;
  if (!asset || typeof asset.browser_download_url !== 'string' || !asset.browser_download_url.startsWith(DOWNLOAD_HOST_PREFIX)) return null;
  const size = Number(asset.size);
  if (!Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0 || size > MAX_ASSET_BYTES) return null;
  const page = typeof json.html_url === 'string' && json.html_url.startsWith(DOWNLOAD_HOST_PREFIX) ? json.html_url : null;
  return { version: m[1], url: asset.browser_download_url, size, page };
}

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

/** Transient = worth retrying (network error, timeout, 5xx). */
class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(status) {
    super(`HTTP ${status}`);
    this.status = status;
    this.transient = status >= 500;
  }
}

async function fetchJson(fetchFn, url, timeoutMs) {
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const res = await fetchFn(url, {
      signal: ctrl.signal,
      headers: { Accept: 'application/vnd.github+json', 'User-Agent': 'Crosshair-Z' },
    });
    if (res.status === 404) return null; // no published release yet
    if (!res.ok) throw new HttpError(res.status);
    return await res.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

/**
 * Ask GitHub for the latest release. Never throws.
 * @param {{fetchFn: typeof fetch, currentVersion: string, log: object, retryBaseMs?: number}} opts
 * @returns {Promise<{version: string, url: string, size: number, page: string|null} | null>} newer release or null
 */
async function checkForUpdate({ fetchFn, currentVersion, log, retryBaseMs = RETRY_BASE_MS }) {
  const started = Date.now();
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= CHECK_RETRIES; attempt++) {
    try {
      const json = await fetchJson(fetchFn, RELEASES_URL, CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS);
      const rel = json && parseRelease(json);
      if (json && !rel) log.warn('update check: unusable release payload', { tag: json.tag_name });
      const newer = rel && compareVersions(rel.version, currentVersion) > 0 ? rel : null;
      log.info('update check done', { current: currentVersion, latest: rel && rel.version, newer: !!newer, ms: Date.now() - started });
      return newer;
    } catch (err) {
      const transient = !(err instanceof HttpError) || err.transient;
      log.warn('update check failed', { attempt, transient, error: err.message });
      if (!transient || attempt === CHECK_RETRIES) return null;
      await sleep(retryBaseMs * 2 ** attempt);
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * Stream the release exe next to the running one as "Crosshair Z <version>.exe".
 * Writes to a .part file and renames only after the size matches. Throws on failure.
 * @returns {Promise<string>} path of the downloaded exe
 */
async function downloadUpdate({ fetchFn, release, dir, log, timeoutMs = DOWNLOAD_TIMEOUT_MS }) {
  if (!release || !VERSION_RE.test(release.version)) throw new Error('invalid release');
  const dest = path.join(dir, `Crosshair Z ${release.version}.exe`);
  const part = `${dest}.part`;
  const ctrl = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), timeoutMs);
  const started = Date.now();
  log.info('update download start', { version: release.version, dest });
  try {
    const res = await fetchFn(release.url, { signal: ctrl.signal, headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Crosshair-Z' } });
    if (!res.ok || !res.body) throw new HttpError(res.status);
    await pipeline(Readable.fromWeb(res.body), fs.createWriteStream(part), { signal: ctrl.signal });
    const { size } = await fs.promises.stat(part);
    if (size !== release.size) throw new Error(`size mismatch: got ${size}, expected ${release.size}`);
    await fs.promises.rename(part, dest);
    log.info('update download done', { dest, bytes: size, ms: Date.now() - started });
    return dest;
  } catch (err) {
    await fs.promises.rm(part, { force: true }).catch((rmErr) => log.warn('update cleanup failed', { error: rmErr.message }));
    log.error('update download failed', { version: release.version, error: err.message });
    throw err;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

module.exports = { compareVersions, parseRelease, checkForUpdate, downloadUpdate, RELEASES_URL, ASSET_NAME };

`src/renderer/common.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/* Shared renderer helpers (window.CZUI). */
(function () {
  const TOAST_MS = 2200;
  let toastTimer = null;

  /**
   * Create element. attrs: class, on<event> handlers, other attributes.
   * @returns {HTMLElement}
   */
  function el(tag, attrs, ...children) {
    const node = document.createElement(tag);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs || {})) {
      if (k === 'class') node.className = v;
      else if (k.startsWith('on')) node.addEventListener(k.slice(2), v);
      else if (v !== undefined && v !== null) node.setAttribute(k, v);
    }
    for (const c of children) if (c != null) node.append(c);
    return node;
  }

  function getPath(obj, path) {
    return path.split('.').reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
  }

  function setPath(obj, path, value) {
    const keys = path.split('.');
    const last = keys.pop();
    keys.reduce((o, k) => o[k], obj)[last] = value;
  }

  function toast(msg, isErr) {
    const t = document.getElementById('toast');
    if (!t) return;
    t.textContent = msg;
    t.className = 'toast show' + (isErr ? ' err' : '');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => (t.className = 'toast'), TOAST_MS);
  }

  /** IPC wrapper: shows toast on failure, never throws; returns null on error. */
  async function call(channel, payload) {
    try {
      return await window.cz.invoke(channel, payload);
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = String(err && err.message ? err.message : err).replace(/^Error invoking remote method '[^']+': (Error: )?/, '');
      console.error(channel, err);
      toast(msg, true);
      return null;
    }
  }

  /** Inline SVG icon from the page sprite (#i-<name>). */
  function icon(name) {
    const NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
    const svg = document.createElementNS(NS, 'svg');
    const use = document.createElementNS(NS, 'use');
    use.setAttribute('href', `#i-${name}`);
    svg.append(use);
    return svg;
  }

  function clone(o) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  }

  // ---------- tooltips ----------
  // Replaces native title tooltips app-wide: any element with title="Text (Ctrl+Z)" gets a styled tip,
  // a trailing "(shortcut)" is shown as a key cap. Delegated, so elements created later work too.

  const TIP_DELAY_MS = 380;
  const TIP_GAP_PX = 8;
  const SHORTCUT_RE = /^(.*\S)\s+\(((?:Ctrl|Shift|Alt|Del|Esc)[^)]{0,12}|[^\s)]{1,2})\)$/;

  function initTooltips() {
    const tip = el('div', { class: 'tip', id: 'czTooltip', role: 'tooltip' });
    document.body.append(tip);
    let target = null;
    let timer = null;

    const prepare = (node) => {
      if (!node || !node.matches) return;
      if (node.hasAttribute('title')) {
        node.dataset.tip = node.getAttribute('title');
        node.removeAttribute('title');
      }
      if (node.dataset.tip && !node.hasAttribute('aria-label') && !node.textContent.trim()) {
        node.setAttribute('aria-label', node.dataset.tip);
      }
    };

    const showFor = (node) => {
      prepare(node);
      if (!node || !node.dataset.tip || node === target) return;
      hide();
      target = node;
      const m = SHORTCUT_RE.exec(node.dataset.tip);
      tip.replaceChildren(m ? m[1] : node.dataset.tip, m ? el('kbd', null, m[2]) : '');
      if (!node.hasAttribute('aria-describedby')) node.setAttribute('aria-describedby', tip.id);
      timer = setTimeout(place, TIP_DELAY_MS);
    };

    const hide = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (target && target.getAttribute('aria-describedby') === tip.id) target.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
      target = null;
      tip.classList.remove('show');
    };

    const place = () => {
      if (!target || !target.isConnected) return hide();
      const r = target.getBoundingClientRect();
      const t = tip.getBoundingClientRect();
      const below = r.bottom + TIP_GAP_PX + t.height < window.innerHeight;
      const x = Math.max(6, Math.min(window.innerWidth - t.width - 6, r.left + r.width / 2 - t.width / 2));
      tip.style.left = `${Math.round(x)}px`;
      tip.style.top = `${Math.round(below ? r.bottom + TIP_GAP_PX : r.top - TIP_GAP_PX - t.height)}px`;
      tip.classList.add('show');
    };

    document.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
      const next = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[title], [data-tip]');
      if (next) showFor(next);
    });
    document.addEventListener('mouseout', (e) => {
      if (target && target.contains(e.target) && !target.contains(e.relatedTarget)) hide();
    });
    document.addEventListener('focusin', (e) => {
      const next = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[title], [data-tip]');
      if (next) showFor(next);
    });
    document.addEventListener('focusout', (e) => {
      if (target && (e.target === target || target.contains(e.target)) && !target.contains(e.relatedTarget)) hide();
    });
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', hide, true);
    document.addEventListener('scroll', hide, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', hide);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initTooltips);
  else initTooltips();

  window.CZUI = { el, icon, getPath, setPath, toast, call, clone };
})();

```

.slide{background:#282d35;min-height:130px}.card.active{border-color:#7cc4ff}.side-item.active{background:#30363f;color:#7cc4ff}.page-pad{padding:26px
