
`src/renderer/settings.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/* Settings window shell: library, saved, position, toggle, hotkeys, share, settings. Designer lives in designer.js. */
(function () {
  const C = CrosshairCore;
  const { el, icon, toast, call } = CZUI;
  const PUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 40;
  // Card preview buffer in device pixels; drawn 1:1 like in-game (no upscaling).
  const CARD_W = 128;
  const CARD_H = 96;
  const MAX_OFFSET = 4000;
  const HOTKEY_LABELS = {
    toggle: 'Show / hide',
    next: 'Next crosshair',
    prev: 'Previous crosshair',
    settings: 'Open app',
    moveUp: 'Move up',
    moveDown: 'Move down',
    moveLeft: 'Move left',
    moveRight: 'Move right',
  };
  const KB_MODES = [
    { v: 'hide', t: 'Hide crosshair', d: 'Shortcut hides / shows the crosshair.' },
    { v: 'switch', t: 'Switch crosshair', d: 'Shortcut swaps to a secondary saved crosshair.' },
  ];
  const RC_MODES = [
    { v: 'off', t: 'Off', d: 'Right mouse button does nothing.' },
    { v: 'hideHold', t: 'Hide while holding', d: 'Great for scoped weapons with own reticle.' },
    { v: 'hideToggle', t: 'Hide on click', d: 'Single click hides, next click shows.' },
    { v: 'switchHold', t: 'Switch while holding', d: 'Secondary crosshair while button held.' },
    { v: 'switchToggle', t: 'Switch on click', d: 'Single click swaps, next click swaps back.' },
  ];

  /** Rail groups → top tabs. */
  const GROUPS = {
    crosshairs: [['discover', 'Discover'], ['library', 'Browse'], ['saved', 'Saved'], ['import', 'Import'], ['share', 'Share']],
    designer: [['designer', 'Designer']],
    keys: [['hotkeys', 'Hotkeys'], ['toggle', 'Toggle']],
    display: [['position', 'Position and size']],
    app: [['settings', 'Settings']],
  };
  const CX_COLLECTION = 'Crosshair X';
  const CATEGORY_ICONS = {
    All: 'grid', Classic: 'shape', Dot: 'dot', Circle: 'ring', 'T-Shape': 'tshape', Sniper: 'scope',
    Dynamic: 'pulse', Layered: 'stack', Stylized: 'spark', [CX_COLLECTION]: 'import', Workshop: 'share', 'Starter pack': 'reticle',
  };

  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const ui = {
    config: null,
    presets: [],
    hotkeyStatus: {},
    defaults: {},
    runtime: {},
    draft: null, // crosshair being edited (active profile)
    draftId: null,
    category: 'All',
    search: '',
    lastPage: {}, // group → last visited page
    pushTimer: null,
    syncers: [],
    recording: null, // {kind:'global'|'profile', key}
    designer: null,
    cx: null, // last Crosshair X scan result
  };

  function activeProfile() {
    const c = ui.config;
    return c.profiles.find((p) => p.id === c.activeProfileId) || c.profiles[0];
  }

  // ---------- thumbnails ----------

  /** Draw crosshair at 1:1 pixels into small canvas (CSS upscales pixelated); shrink if it does not fit. */
  function drawThumb(canvas, ch, w = 36, h = 36) {
    canvas.width = w;
    canvas.height = h;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    const extent = C.crosshairExtent(ch);
    const fit = Math.min(1, (Math.min(w, h) / 2 - 2) / Math.max(1, extent));
    C.drawCrosshair(ctx, ch, w / 2, h / 2, { scale: fit });
  }

  /** Card preview: true screen size, CSS box = buffer / devicePixelRatio so 1 crosshair px = 1 screen px. */
  function drawCardThumb(canvas, ch) {
    drawThumb(canvas, ch, CARD_W, CARD_H);
    const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    canvas.style.width = `${CARD_W / dpr}px`;
    canvas.style.height = `${CARD_H / dpr}px`;
  }

  /**
   * Crosshair card: preview "slide", name, optional tag/extra row and bookmark button.
   * @param {{onMark?: () => void, marked?: boolean}} [opts]
   */
  function card(ch, name, tag, active, extra, opts = {}) {
    const canvas = el('canvas');
    drawCardThumb(canvas, ch);
    const li = el('li', { class: 'card' + (active ? ' active' : ''), title: name }, el('div', { class: 'slide' }, canvas),
      el('div', { class: 'meta' }, el('span', { class: 'name' }, name), tag ? el('span', { class: 'tag' }, tag) : null), extra);
    if (opts.onMark) {
      const mark = el('button', { class: 'mark' + (opts.marked ? ' on' : ''), title: opts.marked ? 'Already in Saved' : 'Add to Saved' }, icon(opts.marked ? 'bookmarked' : 'bookmark'));
      mark.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        if (!opts.marked) opts.onMark();
      });
      li.append(mark);
    }
    return li;
  }

  /** Fingerprints of saved crosshairs, to show which library items are already saved. */
  function savedPrints() {
    return new Set(ui.config.profiles.map((p) => JSON.stringify(C.sanitizeCrosshair(p.crosshair))));
  }

  // ---------- library / saved ----------

  function renderProfiles() {
    const list = $('profileList');
    list.replaceChildren();
    for (const p of ui.config.profiles) {
      const active = p.id === ui.config.activeProfileId;
      const sc = p.hotkey ? el('div', { class: 'shortcut' }, prettyAccel(p.hotkey)) : null;
      const li = card(p.id === ui.draftId ? ui.draft : p.crosshair, p.name, active ? 'equipped' : '', active, sc);
      li.addEventListener('click', () => activate(p.id));
      li.addEventListener('dblclick', () => showPage('designer'));
      list.append(li);
    }
    $('activeName').textContent = activeProfile().name;
    drawThumb($('currentThumb'), ui.draft || activeProfile().crosshair, 44, 44);
  }

  /**
   * Library entries: shipped starter pack (ui.presets) plus this user's own Crosshair X crosshairs.
   * Crosshair X entries identical to a starter-pack design are not listed twice.
   */
  function libraryItems() {
    const prints = new Set(ui.presets.map((p) => JSON.stringify(p.crosshair)));
    const cx = (ui.cx ? ui.cx.items : [])
      .filter((it) => !prints.has(JSON.stringify(it.crosshair)))
      .map((it) => ({ name: it.name, category: it.source === 'Workshop' ? 'Workshop' : CX_COLLECTION, crosshair: it.crosshair }));
    return [...ui.presets, ...cx];
  }

  function renderPresets() {
    const items = libraryItems();
    const cats = ['All', ...new Set(items.map((p) => p.category))];
    if (!cats.includes(ui.category)) ui.category = 'All';
    $('categoryList').replaceChildren(...cats.map((c) => {
      const count = c === 'All' ? items.length : items.filter((p) => p.category === c).length;
      const b = el('button', { class: 'side-item' + (c === ui.category ? ' active' : '') }, icon(CATEGORY_ICONS[c] || 'reticle'),
        el('span', null, c === 'All' ? 'All crosshairs' : c), el('span', { class: 'n' }, String(count)));
      b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        ui.category = c;
        renderPresets();
      });
      return el('li', null, b);
    }));
    const q = ui.search.trim().toLowerCase();
    const shown = items.filter((p) => (ui.category === 'All' || p.category === ui.category)
      && (!q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.category.toLowerCase().includes(q)));
    $('libraryTitle').textContent = ui.category === 'All' ? 'All crosshairs' : ui.category;
    $('libraryEmpty').textContent = items.length
      ? 'No crosshair matches that search.'
      : 'No crosshairs yet. Save some in Crosshair X, or make one with New crosshair under Saved.';
    $('libraryEmpty').classList.toggle('hidden', shown.length > 0);
    const prints = savedPrints();
    $('presetList').replaceChildren(...shown.map((p) => {
      const marked = prints.has(JSON.stringify(p.crosshair));
      const li = card(p.crosshair, p.name, ui.category === 'All' && p.category !== 'Starter pack' ? p.category : '', false, null, {
        marked,
        onMark: () => saveProfile(p.name, p.crosshair),
      });
      li.addEventListener('click', () => createProfile(p.name, p.crosshair, true));
      return li;
    }));
  }

  /** Add to Saved without equipping. */
  async function saveProfile(name, crosshair) {
    flushPush();
    const res = await call('profile:create', { name, crosshair, activate: false, dedupe: true });
    if (res) {
      applyConfig(res.config, false);
      toast(res.existed ? `"${name}" is already in Saved` : `Saved "${name}"`);
    }
  }

  async function activate(id) {
    flushPush();
    const cfg = await call('profile:activate', id);
    if (cfg) applyConfig(cfg, true);
  }

  /** Create + equip. dedupe=true reuses an identical saved crosshair (library clicks). */
  async function createProfile(name, crosshair, dedupe = false) {
    flushPush();
    const res = await call('profile:create', { name, crosshair, dedupe });
    if (res) {
      applyConfig(res.config, true);
      toast(`Equipped "${name}"`);
    }
  }

  async function moveProfile(dir) {
    const cfg = await call('profile:move', { id: ui.config.activeProfileId, dir });
    if (cfg) applyConfig(cfg, false);
  }

  async function deleteProfile() {
    const p = activeProfile();
    if (!window.confirm(`Delete "${p.name}"?`)) return;
    clearTimeout(ui.pushTimer);
    ui.pushTimer = null;
    const cfg = await call('profile:delete', p.id);
    if (cfg) applyConfig(cfg, true);
  }

  // ---------- designer bridge ----------

  function onDesignerChange(draft) {
    ui.draft = draft;
    ui.designer.setStatus('saving');
    schedulePush();
    updateShare();
    const li = $('profileList').querySelector('li.active canvas');
    if (li) drawCardThumb(li, ui.draft);
    drawThumb($('currentThumb'), ui.draft, 44, 44);
  }

  function schedulePush() {
    clearTimeout(ui.pushTimer);
    ui.pushTimer = setTimeout(flushPush, PUSH_DEBOUNCE_MS);
  }

  function flushPush() {
    if (!ui.pushTimer) return;
    clearTimeout(ui.pushTimer);
    ui.pushTimer = null;
    call('profile:update', { id: ui.draftId, crosshair: ui.draft }).then((res) => {
      // only report saved when nothing newer is queued; failures already toast via call()
      if (res && !ui.pushTimer) ui.designer.setStatus('saved');
    });
  }

  async function rename(name) {
    flushPush();
    const cfg = await call('profile:update', { id: ui.draftId, name });
    if (cfg) applyConfig(cfg, false);
  }

  async function copyShareCode() {
    try {
      const code = C.encodeShareCode(ui.draft);
      if ((await call('clipboard:write', code)) !== null) {
        toast(C.hasImageLayers(ui.draft) ? 'Code copied (image layers skipped)' : 'Share code copied');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
    }
  }

  // ---------- position ----------

  /** Slider + number box bound to getter/setter. */
  function sliderRow(label, spec, step, get, set) {
    const range = el('input', { type: 'range', min: spec.min, max: spec.max, step });
    const num = el('input', { type: 'number', min: spec.min, max: spec.max, step });
    const commit = (v) => {
      const n = Number(v);
      if (v !== '' && Number.isFinite(n)) set(n);
    };
    // --p drives the filled part of the track (CSS gradient)
    const paint = () => range.style.setProperty('--p', `${((range.value - spec.min) / (spec.max - spec.min)) * 100}%`);
    range.addEventListener('input', () => {
      paint();
      commit(range.value);
    });
    num.addEventListener('change', () => commit(num.value));
    ui.syncers.push(() => {
      const v = get();
      range.value = v;
      paint();
      if (document.activeElement !== num) num.value = v;
    });
    return el('div', { class: 'ctrl' }, el('label', null, label), range, num);
  }

  function buildPosition() {
    $('sizeControls').append(sliderRow('Size', { min: 0.25, max: 4 }, 0.05, () => ui.config.display.scale, (n) => patch({ display: { scale: n } })));
    for (const axis of ['offsetX', 'offsetY']) {
      $('offsetControls').append(sliderRow(axis === 'offsetX' ? 'X' : 'Y', { min: -500, max: 500 }, 1,
        () => ui.config.display[axis], (n) => patch({ display: { [axis]: Math.max(-MAX_OFFSET, Math.min(MAX_OFFSET, Math.round(n))) } })));
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-nudge]').forEach((b) =>
      b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const [dx, dy] = b.dataset.nudge.split(',').map(Number);
        patch({ display: { offsetX: ui.config.display.offsetX + dx, offsetY: ui.config.display.offsetY + dy } });
      }),
    );
    $('btnCenter').addEventListener('click', () => patch({ display: { offsetX: 0, offsetY: 0 } }));
  }

  async function patch(p) {
    const cfg = await call('config:patch', p);
    if (cfg) applyConfig(cfg, false);
  }

  async function loadMonitors() {
    const list = (await call('displays:list')) || [];
    const sel = $('monitorSelect');
    sel.replaceChildren(el('option', { value: '' }, 'Primary display'), ...list.map((d) => el('option', { value: String(d.id) }, d.label)));
    sel.value = ui.config.display.monitorId == null ? '' : String(ui.config.display.monitorId);
  }

  // ---------- toggle page ----------

  function optionGrid(rootId, modes, current, onPick) {
    $(rootId).replaceChildren(...modes.map((m) =>
      el('button', { class: 'option' + (m.v === current ? ' on' : ''), onclick: () => onPick(m.v) }, el('b', null, m.t), el('span', null, m.d))));
  }

  function profileSelect(sel, value) {
    const opts = ui.config.profiles.map((p) => el('option', { value: p.id }, p.name));
    sel.replaceChildren(el('option', { value: '' }, '— choose saved crosshair —'), ...opts);
    sel.value = value || '';
  }

  const UPDATE_TEXT = {
    idle: () => '',
    checking: () => 'Checking for updates…',
    none: (u) => `You have the latest version (${u.current}).`,
    available: (u) => `Version ${u.version} is available (you have ${u.current}).`,
    downloading: (u) => `Downloading ${u.version}… Crosshair Z restarts on its own when done.`,
    launched: (u) => `Starting ${u.version}…`,
    error: (u) => u.error || 'Update failed.',
  };

  /** Updates block + rail badge, driven by runtime.update from main. */
  function renderUpdate() {
    const u = (ui.runtime && ui.runtime.update) || { status: 'idle' };
    const text = UPDATE_TEXT[u.status] || UPDATE_TEXT.idle;
    $('updateStatus').textContent = text(u);
    $('updateStatus').classList.toggle('err', u.status === 'error');
    const install = $('btnUpdateInstall');
    install.classList.toggle('hidden', u.status !== 'available');
    install.textContent = u.version ? `Update to ${u.version}` : 'Update';
    $('btnUpdateCheck').disabled = ['checking', 'downloading', 'launched'].includes(u.status);
    const rail = document.querySelector('.rail-btn[data-group="app"]');
    if (rail) rail.classList.toggle('has-update', u.status === 'available');
  }

  function renderToggle() {
    const c = ui.config;
    optionGrid('kbModes', KB_MODES, c.toggle.mode, (mode) => patch({ toggle: { mode } }));
    optionGrid('rcModes', RC_MODES, c.rightClick.mode, (mode) => patch({ rightClick: { mode } }));
    profileSelect($('kbProfile'), c.toggle.profileId);
    profileSelect($('rcProfile'), c.rightClick.profileId);
    $('kbProfileField').classList.toggle('hidden', c.toggle.mode !== 'switch');
    $('rcProfileField').classList.toggle('hidden', !c.rightClick.mode.startsWith('switch'));
    $('toggleKeyLabel').textContent = prettyAccel(c.hotkeys.toggle);
    $('mouseWarn').classList.toggle('hidden', ui.runtime.mouseAvailable !== false);
  }

  // ---------- hotkeys ----------

  const MOD_KEYS = new Set(['Control', 'Shift', 'Alt', 'Meta']);
  const KEY_MAP = {
    ' ': 'Space', ArrowUp: 'Up', ArrowDown: 'Down', ArrowLeft: 'Left', ArrowRight: 'Right',
    '+': 'Plus', Escape: 'Esc', Enter: 'Return',
  };

  /** KeyboardEvent → Electron accelerator, or null if only modifiers. */
  function toAccelerator(e) {
    if (MOD_KEYS.has(e.key)) return null;
    let key;
    if (/^Key[A-Z]$/.test(e.code)) key = e.code.slice(3);
    else if (/^Digit[0-9]$/.test(e.code)) key = e.code.slice(5);
    else if (/^Numpad[0-9]$/.test(e.code)) key = 'num' + e.code.slice(6);
    else if (/^F([1-9]|1[0-9]|2[0-4])$/.test(e.key)) key = e.key;
    else key = KEY_MAP[e.key] || (e.key.length === 1 ? e.key.toUpperCase() : e.key);
    const mods = [];
    if (e.ctrlKey) mods.push('CommandOrControl');
    if (e.altKey) mods.push('Alt');
    if (e.shiftKey) mods.push('Shift');
    return [...mods, key].join('+');
  }

  function prettyAccel(a) {
    return a ? a.replace('CommandOrControl', 'Ctrl').replace(/\+/g, ' + ') : '— none —';
  }

  function hotkeyRow(label, accel, status, rec) {
    const recording = ui.recording && ui.recording.kind === rec.kind && ui.recording.key === rec.key;
    const btn = el('button', { class: 'hk-btn' + (recording ? ' recording' : ''), onclick: () => startRecording(rec) }, recording ? 'Press keys…' : prettyAccel(accel));
    const ok = status === 'ok';
    const shown = status && status !== 'empty';
    const badge = el('span', { class: 'badge' + (shown ? (ok ? ' ok' : ' bad') : ''), title: status || '' }, shown ? (ok ? 'active' : status) : '');
    return el('div', { class: 'hk-row' }, el('span', { class: 'hk-name', title: label }, label), btn, badge);
  }

  function renderHotkeys() {
    const row = (k) => hotkeyRow(HOTKEY_LABELS[k], ui.config.hotkeys[k], ui.hotkeyStatus[k], { kind: 'global', key: k });
    $('hotkeyRows').replaceChildren(row('toggle'));
    $('moreHotkeyRows').replaceChildren(...Object.keys(HOTKEY_LABELS).filter((k) => k !== 'toggle').map(row));
    $('profileHotkeyRows').replaceChildren(...ui.config.profiles.map((p) =>
      hotkeyRow(p.name, p.hotkey, ui.hotkeyStatus[`profile:${p.id}`], { kind: 'profile', key: p.id })));
  }

  async function startRecording(rec) {
    ui.recording = rec;
    await call('hotkeys:suspend', true);
    renderHotkeys();
  }

  async function stopRecording(accel) {
    const rec = ui.recording;
    ui.recording = null;
    if (accel !== undefined && rec) {
      if (rec.kind === 'global') {
        const status = await call('hotkeys:set', { [rec.key]: accel });
        if (status) ui.hotkeyStatus = status;
      } else {
        const cfg = await call('profile:update', { id: rec.key, hotkey: accel });
        if (cfg) ui.config = cfg;
      }
    }
    await call('hotkeys:suspend', false);
    renderHotkeys();
    renderToggle();
  }

  function onRecordKey(e) {
    if (!ui.recording) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.key === 'Escape') return stopRecording();
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' || e.key === 'Delete') return stopRecording('');
    const accel = toAccelerator(e);
    if (accel) stopRecording(accel);
  }

  // ---------- share ----------

  function updateShare() {
    let code = '';
    try {
      code = C.encodeShareCode(ui.draft);
    } catch (err) {
      code = err.message;
    }
    $('exportCode').value = code;
    $('shareImageWarn').classList.toggle('hidden', !C.hasImageLayers(ui.draft));
  }

  async function importCode() {
    let ch;
    try {
      ch = C.decodeShareCode($('importCode').value);
    } catch (err) {
      toast(err.message, true);
      return;
    }
    await createProfile('Imported', ch);
    $('importCode').value = '';
  }

  // ---------- Crosshair X import ----------

  function renderImport(res) {
    ui.cx = res;
    renderPresets();
    const list = $('cxList');
    const n = res.items.length;
    $('cxStatus').textContent = n
      ? `Found ${n} crosshair${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}${res.errors.length ? ` (${res.errors.length} could not be read)` : ''}. They also appear in the library under ${CX_COLLECTION}.`
      : res.exists === false ? 'Crosshair X is not installed for this Windows user. Use Import file to pick a crosshair .json.' : 'Crosshair X has no saved crosshairs yet.';
    $('cxImportAll').disabled = n === 0;
    list.replaceChildren(...res.items.map((it) => {
      const extra = it.warnings.length ? el('div', { class: 'shortcut', title: it.warnings.join(', ') }, `Converted with ${it.warnings.length} change${it.warnings.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}`) : null;
      const li = card(it.crosshair, it.name, it.source, false, extra);
      li.addEventListener('click', () => importCx([it]));
      return li;
    }));
  }

  async function scanCx() {
    $('cxStatus').textContent = 'Scanning…';
    const res = await call('cx:scan');
    if (res) renderImport(res);
  }

  async function importCx(items) {
    flushPush();
    const res = await call('profile:importMany', items.map((it) => ({ name: it.name, crosshair: it.crosshair })));
    if (!res) return;
    applyConfig(res.config, true);
    const notes = [];
    if (res.duplicates) notes.push(`${res.duplicates} already saved`);
    if (res.overLimit) notes.push(`${res.overLimit} over the limit`);
    toast(`Imported ${res.imported} crosshair${res.imported === 1 ? '' : 's'}${notes.length ? ` (${notes.join(', ')})` : ''}`);
  }

  async function removeDuplicates() {
    flushPush();
    const res = await call('profile:dedupe');
    if (!res) return;
    applyConfig(res.config, true);
    toast(res.removed ? `Removed ${res.removed} duplicate${res.removed === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : 'No duplicates found');
  }

  async function importCxFile() {
    const res = await call('cx:openFile');
    if (!res) return;
    if (res.errors.length) toast(`${res.errors[0].file}: ${res.errors[0].error}`, true);
    if (res.items.length) await importCx(res.items);
  }

  // ---------- config application ----------

  /**
   * Take config from main. resetDraft=true reloads designer from active profile.
   * @param {object} cfg
   * @param {boolean} resetDraft
   */
  function applyConfig(cfg, resetDraft) {
    ui.config = cfg;
    const active = activeProfile();
    const switched = active.id !== ui.draftId;
    if (resetDraft || switched || !ui.pushTimer) {
      ui.draftId = active.id;
      ui.draft = C.sanitizeCrosshair(active.crosshair);
      ui.designer.load(ui.draft, active.name, resetDraft || switched);
    } else {
      ui.designer.redraw();
    }
    $('enabledToggle').checked = cfg.enabled;
    for (const k of ['launchAtLogin', 'startHidden', 'hideOnLaunch', 'checkUpdates']) $(k).checked = cfg[k];
    const sel = $('monitorSelect');
    if (sel.options.length) sel.value = cfg.display.monitorId == null ? '' : String(cfg.display.monitorId);
    for (const s of ui.syncers) s();
    updateShare();
    renderProfiles();
    renderPresets();
    renderToggle();
    if (!ui.recording) renderHotkeys();
  }

  // ---------- wiring ----------

  function groupOf(page) {
    return Object.keys(GROUPS).find((g) => GROUPS[g].some(([p]) => p === page)) || 'crosshairs';
  }

  function showPage(page) {
    if (page === 'discover' && ui.config) {
      $('discoverCards').replaceChildren(...ui.presets.slice(0, 10).map(p => {
        const item = card(p.crosshair, p.name, p.category, false);
        item.addEventListener('click', () => createProfile(p.name, p.crosshair));
        return item;
      }));
    }
    const group = groupOf(page);
    ui.lastPage[group] = page;
    document.querySelectorAll('.rail-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.group === group));
    // a section with a single page needs no tab strip (its page has its own header/toolbar)
    document.querySelector('.shell').classList.toggle('single', GROUPS[group].length === 1);
    $('tabs').replaceChildren(...GROUPS[group].map(([p, label]) =>
      el('button', { class: 'tab' + (p === page ? ' active' : ''), role: 'tab', onclick: () => showPage(p) }, label)));
    document.querySelectorAll('.page').forEach((p) => p.classList.toggle('hidden', p.dataset.page !== page));
    if (page === 'position') loadMonitors();
    if (page === 'library') $('librarySearch').focus({ preventScroll: true });
    if (page === 'designer') ui.designer.resize();
  }

  function wire() {
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-goto]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => showPage(b.dataset.goto)));
    document.querySelectorAll('.rail-btn').forEach((b) => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const g = b.dataset.group;
      showPage(ui.lastPage[g] || GROUPS[g][0][0]);
    }));
    $('librarySearch').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      ui.search = e.target.value;
      renderPresets();
    });
    // used by capture mode; "designer:light" also picks a stage background
    window.czShowPage = (spec) => {
      const [page, bg] = String(spec).split(":");
      showPage(page);
      const btn = bg && document.querySelector(`#dzBg [data-bg="${bg}"]`);
      if (btn) btn.click();
    };
    $('btnNew').addEventListener('click', () => createProfile('New Crosshair', C.DEFAULT_CROSSHAIR));
    $('btnDup').addEventListener('click', () => createProfile(activeProfile().name + ' copy', ui.draft));
    $('btnDel').addEventListener('click', deleteProfile);
    $('btnDedupe').addEventListener('click', removeDuplicates);
    $('btnUp').addEventListener('click', () => moveProfile(-1));
    $('btnDown').addEventListener('click', () => moveProfile(1));

    $('monitorSelect').addEventListener('change', (e) => patch({ display: { monitorId: e.target.value === '' ? null : Number(e.target.value) } }));
    $('kbProfile').addEventListener('change', (e) => patch({ toggle: { profileId: e.target.value || null } }));
    $('rcProfile').addEventListener('change', (e) => patch({ rightClick: { profileId: e.target.value || null } }));
    for (const key of ['launchAtLogin', 'startHidden', 'hideOnLaunch', 'checkUpdates']) {
      $(key).addEventListener('change', (e) => patch({ [key]: e.target.checked }));
    }
    $('enabledToggle').addEventListener('change', (e) => patch({ enabled: e.target.checked }));

    $('btnUpdateCheck').addEventListener('click', () => call('update:check'));
    $('btnUpdateInstall').addEventListener('click', () => call('update:install'));

    $('btnHotkeyDefaults').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const status = await call('hotkeys:set', ui.defaults);
      if (status) ui.hotkeyStatus = status;
    });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onRecordKey, true);
    window.addEventListener('blur', () => ui.recording && stopRecording());

    $('btnCopy').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      if ((await call('clipboard:write', $('exportCode').value)) !== null) toast('Code copied');
    });
    $('btnPaste').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const text = await call('clipboard:read');
      if (text != null) $('importCode').value = text;
    });
    $('btnImport').addEventListener('click', importCode);
    $('cxRescan').addEventListener('click', scanCx);
    $('cxFile').addEventListener('click', importCxFile);
    $('cxImportAll').addEventListener('click', () => ui.cx && ui.cx.items.length && importCx(ui.cx.items));

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', flushPush);
    window.cz.on('config:changed', (cfg) => applyConfig(cfg, false));
    window.cz.on('runtime:changed', (rt) => {
      ui.runtime = rt || {};
      renderToggle();
      renderUpdate();
    });
    window.cz.on('hotkeys:status', (s) => {
      ui.hotkeyStatus = s || {};
      if (!ui.recording) renderHotkeys();
    });
  }

  async function init() {
    ui.designer = CZDesigner.create({
      onChange: onDesignerChange,
      onRename: rename,
      onSave: () => {
        flushPush();
        toast('Saved');
      },
      onShare: copyShareCode,
      getScale: () => (ui.config ? ui.config.display.scale : 1),
      onImageLoaded: () => ui.config && renderProfiles(),
    });
    wire();
    buildPosition();
    const res = await call('config:get');
    if (!res) return;
    ui.presets = res.presets || [];
    ui.hotkeyStatus = res.hotkeyStatus || {};
    ui.runtime = res.runtime || {};
    ui.defaults = res.defaults || {};
    applyConfig(res.config, true);
    renderUpdate();
    showPage('discover');
    loadMonitors();
    scanCx();
    const logPath = await call('log:path');
    if (logPath) $('logPath').textContent = logPath;
  }

  init();
})();

```

`src/shared/crosshair-core.js`:

```js
/**
 * Crosshair Z core: schema, validation, layered rendering, firing animation, share codes.
 * UMD module — used by main process (require) and renderers (<script>).
 */
(function (root, factory) {
  const api = factory();
  if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
  else root.CrosshairCore = api;
})(typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : this, function () {
  'use strict';

  const SHARE_PREFIX = 'CZ2-';
  const LEGACY_SHARE_PREFIX = 'CZ1-';
  const COLOR_RE = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/;
  const IMAGE_RE = /^data:image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/;
  const MAX_IMAGE_CHARS = 1500000;
  const MAX_LAYERS = 8;
  const OVERLAY_PADDING_PX = 4;
  const MIN_OVERLAY_PX = 32;
  const MAX_IMAGE_CACHE = 32;

  /** Leaf specs: n = number, b = boolean, c = hex color, e = enum, s = string, img = image data URL. */
  const LAYER_SCHEMA = {
    name: { t: 's', max: 24 },
    visible: { t: 'b' },
    kind: { t: 'e', values: ['shape', 'image'] },
    offsetX: { t: 'n', min: -300, max: 300, int: true },
    offsetY: { t: 'n', min: -300, max: 300, int: true },
    color: { t: 'c' },
    opacity: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 1 },
    rotation: { t: 'n', min: -180, max: 180, int: true },
    scale: { t: 'n', min: 0.25, max: 8 },
    blur: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 10, int: true },
    lines: {
      enabled: { t: 'b' },
      length: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 100, int: true },
      thickness: { t: 'n', min: 1, max: 20, int: true },
      gap: { t: 'n', min: -20, max: 60, int: true },
      top: { t: 'b' },
      bottom: { t: 'b' },
      left: { t: 'b' },
      right: { t: 'b' },
    },
    outline: {
      enabled: { t: 'b' },
      thickness: { t: 'n', min: 1, max: 6, int: true },
      color: { t: 'c' },
      opacity: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 1 },
    },
    dot: {
      enabled: { t: 'b' },
      size: { t: 'n', min: 1, max: 20, int: true },
      shape: { t: 'e', values: ['square', 'circle'] },
      color: { t: 'c' },
      useMainColor: { t: 'b' },
    },
    circle: {
      enabled: { t: 'b' },
      radius: { t: 'n', min: 1, max: 120, int: true },
      thickness: { t: 'n', min: 1, max: 12, int: true },
    },
    image: {
      src: { t: 'img' },
      size: { t: 'n', min: 4, max: 512, int: true },
    },
    firing: {
      enabled: { t: 'b' },
      spread: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 60, int: true },
      kick: { t: 'n', min: 0, max: 60, int: true },
      speed: { t: 'n', min: 20, max: 1000, int: true },
    },
  };

  const DEFAULT_LAYER = deepFreeze({
    name: 'Layer 1',
    visible: true,
    kind: 'shape',
    offsetX: 0,
    offsetY: 0,
    color: '#00ff66',
    opacity: 1,
    rotation: 0,
    scale: 1,
    blur: 0,
    lines: { enabled: true, length: 6, thickness: 2, gap: 3, top: true, bottom: true, left: true, right: true },
    outline: { enabled: true, thickness: 1, color: '#000000', opacity: 1 },
    dot: { enabled: false, size: 2, shape: 'square', color: '#ffffff', useMainColor: true },
    circle: { enabled: false, radius: 12, thickness: 1 },
    image: { src: '', size: 64 },
    firing: { enabled: false, spread: 6, kick: 0, speed: 120 },
  });

  const DEFAULT_CROSSHAIR = deepFreeze({ layers: [DEFAULT_LAYER] });

  function deepFreeze(o) {
    for (const v of Object.values(o)) if (v && typeof v === 'object') deepFreeze(v);
    return Object.freeze(o);
  }

  function clone(o) {
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  }

  // ---------- validation ----------

  function isLeaf(node) {
    return node && typeof node.t === 'string';
  }

  function sanitizeLeaf(spec, value, fallback) {
    switch (spec.t) {
      case 'b':
        return typeof value === 'boolean' ? value : fallback;
      case 'c':
        return typeof value === 'string' && COLOR_RE.test(value) ? value.toLowerCase() : fallback;
      case 'e':
        return spec.values.includes(value) ? value : fallback;
      case 's':
        return typeof value === 'string' ? value.replace(/[\r\n\t]/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, spec.max) || fallback : fallback;
      case 'img':
        return typeof value === 'string' && value.length <= MAX_IMAGE_CHARS && IMAGE_RE.test(value) ? value : fallback;
      case 'n': {
        const num = typeof value === 'number' ? value : Number(value);
        if (value === null || value === '' || !Number.isFinite(num)) return fallback;
        const clamped = Math.min(spec.max, Math.max(spec.min, num));
        return spec.int ? Math.round(clamped) : Math.round(clamped * 1000) / 1000;
      }
      default:
        return fallback;
    }
  }

  function sanitizeNode(schema, defaults, input) {
    const src = input && typeof input === 'object' ? input : {};
    const out = {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(schema)) {
      out[key] = isLeaf(schema[key])
        ? sanitizeLeaf(schema[key], src[key], defaults[key])
        : sanitizeNode(schema[key], defaults[key], src[key]);
    }
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * Validate one layer; unknown keys dropped, bad values replaced by defaults.
   * @param {unknown} input
   * @returns {object}
   */
  function sanitizeLayer(input) {
    return sanitizeNode(LAYER_SCHEMA, DEFAULT_LAYER, input);
  }

  /**
   * Validate crosshair. Accepts v2 ({layers}) and legacy v1 (flat single design).
   * @param {unknown} input
   * @returns {{layers: object[]}}
   */
  function sanitizeCrosshair(input) {
    const src = input && typeof input === 'object' ? input : {};
    const rawLayers = Array.isArray(src.layers) ? src.layers : input && typeof input === 'object' && !('layers' in src) ? [src] : [];
    const layers = rawLayers.slice(0, MAX_LAYERS).filter((l) => l && typeof l === 'object').map(sanitizeLayer);
    // Image layer without picture is useless → keep it but it renders nothing; ensure at least one layer.
    if (layers.length === 0) layers.push(clone(DEFAULT_LAYER));
    return { layers };
  }

  // ---------- geometry ----------

  function px(v, s) {
    return Math.max(1, Math.round(v * s));
  }

  /** Max distance (physical px) from layer anchor any pixel reaches, incl. full firing spread. */
  function layerExtent(layer, globalScale) {
    const s = layer.scale * globalScale;
    const o = layer.outline.enabled ? layer.outline.thickness : 0;
    const spread = layer.firing.enabled ? layer.firing.spread : 0;
    let r = 0;
    if (layer.kind === 'image') {
      r = (layer.image.size * s * Math.SQRT2) / 2;
    } else {
      if (layer.lines.enabled) {
        const along = Math.abs(layer.lines.gap + spread) + layer.lines.length;
        r = Math.max(r, Math.hypot(along, layer.lines.thickness / 2) * s + o);
      }
      if (layer.circle.enabled) r = Math.max(r, (layer.circle.radius + spread + layer.circle.thickness) * s + o);
      if (layer.dot.enabled) r = Math.max(r, layer.dot.size * s * Math.SQRT2 + o);
    }
    const kick = layer.firing.enabled ? layer.firing.kick * globalScale : 0;
    const off = Math.hypot(layer.offsetX, layer.offsetY) * globalScale;
    return Math.ceil(r + off + kick + layer.blur * 2);
  }

  /**
   * Max extent over visible layers.
   * @param {object} ch sanitized crosshair
   * @param {number} [globalScale=1]
   */
  function crosshairExtent(ch, globalScale = 1) {
    return ch.layers.reduce((m, l) => (l.visible ? Math.max(m, layerExtent(l, globalScale)) : m), 0);
  }

  /** Overlay canvas edge length in physical px (even). */
  function overlaySizePx(ch, globalScale = 1) {
    const size = Math.max(MIN_OVERLAY_PX, 2 * (crosshairExtent(ch, globalScale) + OVERLAY_PADDING_PX));
    return size + (size % 2);
  }

  /**
   * Arm rects in local coords. Origin sits at pixel center for odd thickness (snap = 0.5),
   * so arms are shifted by snap along their axis to land on whole pixels (no half-pixel AA edges).
   */
  function lineRects(layer, s, extraGap, snap) {
    const L = layer.lines;
    const t = px(L.thickness, s);
    const len = Math.round(L.length * s);
    const gap = Math.round((L.gap + extraGap) * s);
    if (!L.enabled || len <= 0) return [];
    const half = t / 2;
    const rects = [];
    if (L.right) rects.push([gap + snap, -half, len, t]);
    if (L.left) rects.push([-gap - snap - len, -half, len, t]);
    if (L.bottom) rects.push([-half, gap + snap, t, len]);
    if (L.top) rects.push([-half, -gap - snap - len, t, len]);
    return rects;
  }

  // ---------- drawing ----------

  const imageCache = new Map();
  let imageListener = null;

  /** Register callback fired when an image layer finishes decoding (to trigger re-render). */
  function onImageLoaded(cb) {
    imageListener = cb;
  }

  function getImage(src) {
    if (!src || typeof Image === 'undefined') return null;
    let img = imageCache.get(src);
    if (!img) {
      img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => imageListener && imageListener();
      img.onerror = () => console.warn('crosshair image failed to decode');
      img.src = src;
      imageCache.set(src, img);
      if (imageCache.size > MAX_IMAGE_CACHE) imageCache.delete(imageCache.keys().next().value);
    }
    return img.complete && img.naturalWidth ? img : null;
  }

  /** Shape pass with alpha 1. grow > 0 = outline silhouette. */
  function drawShapePass(ctx, layer, s, extraGap, grow, color, dotColor, snap) {
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    for (const [x, y, w, h] of lineRects(layer, s, extraGap, snap)) ctx.fillRect(x - grow, y - grow, w + grow * 2, h + grow * 2);
    if (layer.circle.enabled) {
      ctx.lineWidth = px(layer.circle.thickness, s) + grow * 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(0, 0, (layer.circle.radius + extraGap) * s, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    if (layer.dot.enabled) {
      ctx.fillStyle = dotColor;
      const size = px(layer.dot.size, s) + grow * 2;
      // Origin is at floor+snap; pick rect start so absolute coords are whole pixels
      // even when dot size parity differs from line thickness parity.
      const start = Math.floor(-size / 2 + snap) - snap;
      if (layer.dot.shape === 'circle') {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(start + size / 2, start + size / 2, size / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      } else {
        ctx.fillRect(start, start, size, size);
      }
    }
  }

  /** Scratch canvases reused across frames (keyed by slot). */
  const scratch = [];
  function scratchCtx(slot, w, h) {
    let c = scratch[slot];
    if (!c) {
      c = typeof OffscreenCanvas !== 'undefined' ? new OffscreenCanvas(w, h) : document.createElement('canvas');
      scratch[slot] = c;
    }
    if (c.width !== w || c.height !== h) {
      c.width = w;
      c.height = h;
    }
    const ctx = c.getContext('2d');
    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    return ctx;
  }

  function withAnchor(ctx, x, y, snap, rotation, fn) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(Math.floor(x) + snap, Math.floor(y) + snap);
    if (rotation) ctx.rotate((rotation * Math.PI) / 180);
    fn(ctx);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /**
   * Render one shape layer into scratch at full alpha, then composite once with layer opacity.
   * Prevents darker edges/overlaps when opacity < 1 (uniform transparency).
   */
  function drawShapeLayer(ctx, layer, x, y, s, fire) {
    const { width: w, height: h } = ctx.canvas;
    // Round spread so animated gap stays on whole pixels.
    const extraGap = layer.firing.enabled ? Math.round(layer.firing.spread * fire * s) / s : 0;
    const snap = px(layer.lines.thickness, s) % 2 ? 0.5 : 0;
    const dotColor = layer.dot.useMainColor ? layer.color : layer.dot.color;
    const fillCtx = scratchCtx(0, w, h);
    withAnchor(fillCtx, x, y, snap, layer.rotation, (c) => drawShapePass(c, layer, s, extraGap, 0, layer.color, dotColor, snap));
    const layerCtx = scratchCtx(1, w, h);
    if (layer.outline.enabled) {
      const lineCtx = scratchCtx(2, w, h);
      const o = layer.outline.thickness;
      const oc = layer.outline.color;
      withAnchor(lineCtx, x, y, snap, layer.rotation, (c) => drawShapePass(c, layer, s, extraGap, o, oc, oc, snap));
      // Cut fill area out of outline so semi-transparent outline never shows under fill.
      lineCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
      lineCtx.drawImage(fillCtx.canvas, 0, 0);
      layerCtx.globalAlpha = layer.outline.opacity;
      layerCtx.drawImage(lineCtx.canvas, 0, 0);
      layerCtx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }
    layerCtx.drawImage(fillCtx.canvas, 0, 0);
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = layer.opacity;
    if (layer.blur) ctx.filter = `blur(${layer.blur}px)`;
    ctx.drawImage(layerCtx.canvas, 0, 0);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawImageLayer(ctx, layer, x, y, s) {
    const img = getImage(layer.image.src);
    if (!img) return;
    const size = layer.image.size * s;
    const ratio = img.naturalWidth / img.naturalHeight;
    const w = ratio >= 1 ? size : size * ratio;
    const h = ratio >= 1 ? size / ratio : size;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = layer.opacity;
    if (layer.blur) ctx.filter = `blur(${layer.blur}px)`;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
    withAnchor(ctx, x, y, 0, layer.rotation, (c) => c.drawImage(img, -w / 2, -h / 2, w, h));
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /**
   * Render crosshair centered on (cx, cy) in physical pixel space.
   * @param {CanvasRenderingContext2D} ctx
   * @param {{layers: object[]}} ch sanitized crosshair
   * @param {number} cx
   * @param {number} cy
   * @param {{scale?: number, fire?: number[]}} [opts] global scale; per-layer firing level 0..1
   */
  function drawCrosshair(ctx, ch, cx, cy, opts = {}) {
    const g = opts.scale || 1;
    ch.layers.forEach((layer, i) => {
      if (!layer.visible) return;
      const fire = (opts.fire && opts.fire[i]) || 0;
      const kick = layer.firing.enabled ? layer.firing.kick * fire : 0;
      const x = cx + layer.offsetX * g;
      const y = cy + (layer.offsetY - kick) * g;
      const s = layer.scale * g;
      if (layer.kind === 'image') drawImageLayer(ctx, layer, x, y, s);
      else drawShapeLayer(ctx, layer, x, y, s, fire);
    });
  }

  /**
   * Pixel bounding box of one layer as rendered (alpha > 0), relative to crosshair center.
   * Browser only (needs OffscreenCanvas). Returns null when layer draws nothing.
   * @returns {{x:number,y:number,w:number,h:number}|null}
   */
  function layerBounds(ch, index, opts = {}) {
    if (typeof OffscreenCanvas === 'undefined') return null;
    const l = ch.layers[index];
    if (!l) return null;
    const solo = { layers: ch.layers.map((x, i) => Object.assign({}, x, { visible: i === index })) };
    const size = overlaySizePx(solo, opts.scale || 1);
    const c = new OffscreenCanvas(size, size);
    const ctx = c.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
    drawCrosshair(ctx, solo, size / 2, size / 2, opts);
    const data = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, size, size).data;
    let minX = size, minY = size, maxX = -1, maxY = -1;
    for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        if (data[(y * size + x) * 4 + 3] === 0) continue;
        if (x < minX) minX = x;
        if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
        if (y < minY) minY = y;
        if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
      }
    }
    if (maxX < 0) return null;
    return { x: minX - size / 2, y: minY - size / 2, w: maxX - minX + 1, h: maxY - minY + 1 };
  }

  // ---------- firing animation ----------

  /**
   * Per-layer firing level animator. Level rises toward 1 while firing, falls to 0 after.
   */
  class FireAnimator {
    constructor() {
      this.levels = [];
      this.firing = false;
    }

    setFiring(on) {
      this.firing = !!on;
    }

    /**
     * Advance by dt ms. Returns true while still animating.
     * @param {number} dt
     * @param {{layers: object[]}} ch
     */
    step(dt, ch) {
      let moving = false;
      this.levels.length = ch.layers.length;
      ch.layers.forEach((layer, i) => {
        const cur = this.levels[i] || 0;
        const target = this.firing && layer.firing.enabled ? 1 : 0;
        if (cur === target) {
          this.levels[i] = cur;
          return;
        }
        const delta = dt / layer.firing.speed;
        const next = target > cur ? Math.min(1, cur + delta) : Math.max(0, cur - delta);
        this.levels[i] = next;
        if (next !== target) moving = true;
      });
      return moving;
    }
  }

  function hasFiring(ch) {
    return ch.layers.some((l) => l.visible && l.firing.enabled);
  }

  // ---------- share codes ----------

  function bytesToBase64Url(bytes) {
    let b64;
    if (typeof Buffer !== 'undefined') b64 = Buffer.from(bytes).toString('base64');
    else b64 = btoa(Array.from(bytes, (b) => String.fromCharCode(b)).join(''));
    return b64.replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
  }

  function base64UrlToBytes(str) {
    const b64 = str.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    if (typeof Buffer !== 'undefined') return new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(b64, 'base64'));
    return Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
  }

  /** Deep diff vs defaults → only changed keys (keeps codes short). */
  function diff(obj, def) {
    const out = {};
    for (const k of Object.keys(obj)) {
      if (obj[k] && typeof obj[k] === 'object') {
        const d = diff(obj[k], def[k]);
        if (Object.keys(d).length) out[k] = d;
      } else if (obj[k] !== def[k]) {
        out[k] = obj[k];
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** True if crosshair contains image layers (not shareable). */
  function hasImageLayers(ch) {
    return ch.layers.some((l) => l.kind === 'image');
  }

  /**
   * Encode crosshair as share code. Image layers are dropped (too large to share).
   * Throws if nothing shareable remains.
   * @param {object} ch
   * @returns {string}
   */
  function encodeShareCode(ch) {
    const layers = sanitizeCrosshair(ch).layers.filter((l) => l.kind !== 'image');
    if (!layers.length) throw new Error('Image crosshairs cannot be shared');
    const compact = layers.map((l) => diff(l, DEFAULT_LAYER));
    return SHARE_PREFIX + bytesToBase64Url(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(compact)));
  }

  /**
   * Decode share code (CZ2 or legacy CZ1). Throws Error with readable message when invalid.
   * @param {string} code
   * @returns {{layers: object[]}}
   */
  function decodeShareCode(code) {
    const trimmed = typeof code === 'string' ? code.trim() : '';
    const prefix = [SHARE_PREFIX, LEGACY_SHARE_PREFIX].find((p) => trimmed.startsWith(p));
    if (!prefix) throw new Error('Code must start with ' + SHARE_PREFIX);
    const body = trimmed.slice(prefix.length);
    if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(body)) throw new Error('Code contains invalid characters');
    let parsed;
    try {
      parsed = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(base64UrlToBytes(body)));
    } catch (err) {
      throw new Error('Code is corrupted');
    }
    const ch = sanitizeCrosshair(prefix === SHARE_PREFIX ? { layers: Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [] } : parsed);
    ch.layers.forEach((l) => {
      l.kind = 'shape';
      l.image.src = '';
    });
    return ch;
  }

  // ---------- presets ----------

  function layer(patch) {
    const base = clone(DEFAULT_LAYER);
    for (const k of Object.keys(patch)) {
      base[k] = patch[k] && typeof patch[k] === 'object' ? Object.assign(base[k], patch[k]) : patch[k];
    }
    return base;
  }

  function preset(category, name, ...patches) {
    const layers = patches.length ? patches : [{}];
    return { category, name, crosshair: sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: layers.map((p, i) => layer({ name: `Layer ${i + 1}`, ...p })) }) };
  }

  const NO_LINES = { enabled: false };
  const NO_OUTLINE = { enabled: false };
  const FIRE = (spread, kick = 0, speed = 120) => ({ enabled: true, spread, kick, speed });

  /** Original Crosshair Z library. */
  const PRESETS = [
    preset('Classic', 'Classic Green', {}),
    preset('Classic', 'Classic Cyan', { color: '#00e5ff' }),
    preset('Classic', 'Classic Yellow', { color: '#ffe600' }),
    preset('Classic', 'Classic Magenta', { color: '#ff2bd6' }),
    preset('Classic', 'Plus Dot', { color: '#ffe600', lines: { gap: 4, length: 5 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 } }),
    preset('Classic', 'Wide Gap', { color: '#00ff66', lines: { gap: 8, length: 6 } }),
    preset('Classic', 'Thick Bold', { color: '#ffffff', lines: { thickness: 4, length: 8, gap: 4 }, outline: { thickness: 2 } }),
    preset('Classic', 'No Outline', { color: '#00ffff', outline: NO_OUTLINE }),

    preset('Dot', 'Tiny Dot', { color: '#ff2bd6', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 3 } }),
    preset('Dot', 'Pixel Dot', { color: '#00ff66', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 1 } }),
    preset('Dot', 'Round Dot', { color: '#ff3b3b', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 5, shape: 'circle' } }),
    preset('Dot', 'Big Square', { color: '#ffe600', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 6 } }),
    preset('Dot', 'Ghost Dot', { color: '#ffffff', opacity: 0.6, lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 3, shape: 'circle' }, outline: NO_OUTLINE }),

    preset('Circle', 'Circle Dot', { color: '#ffffff', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2, shape: 'circle' }, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 14 } }),
    preset('Circle', 'Ring', { color: '#00ff66', lines: NO_LINES, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 10, thickness: 2 } }),
    preset('Circle', 'Shotgun Ring', { color: '#ff3b3b', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 }, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 30 } }),
    preset('Circle', 'Scoped Cross', { color: '#00e5ff', lines: { gap: 6, length: 10, thickness: 1 }, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 12 } }),

    preset('T-Shape', 'T-Shape', { color: '#00e5ff', lines: { top: false, length: 7, gap: 4 } }),
    preset('T-Shape', 'T Dot', { color: '#ffe600', lines: { top: false, length: 6, gap: 3 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 } }),
    preset('T-Shape', 'Chevron', { color: '#ff2bd6', rotation: 45, lines: { top: false, left: false, length: 8, gap: 0 } }),
    preset('T-Shape', 'Bottom Only', { color: '#00ff66', lines: { top: false, left: false, right: false, length: 10, gap: 2 } }),

    preset('Sniper', 'Sniper Thin', { color: '#ff3b3b', lines: { thickness: 1, length: 14, gap: 0 }, outline: NO_OUTLINE }),
    preset('Sniper', 'Long Lines', { color: '#00ff66', lines: { thickness: 1, length: 40, gap: 6 } }),
    preset('Sniper', 'Mil-Dot', { color: '#000000', lines: { thickness: 1, length: 30, gap: 2 }, outline: { color: '#ffffff' }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2, shape: 'circle' } }),
    preset('Sniper', 'Fine Point', { color: '#ffffff', lines: { thickness: 1, length: 4, gap: 2 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 1 } }),

    preset('Dynamic', 'Spread Cross', { color: '#00ff66', firing: FIRE(8) }),
    preset('Dynamic', 'Spread Ring', { color: '#00e5ff', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 }, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 8 }, firing: FIRE(10, 0, 160) }),
    preset('Dynamic', 'Recoil Kick', { color: '#ffe600', lines: { gap: 3, length: 5 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 }, firing: FIRE(3, 6, 90) }),
    preset('Dynamic', 'Static Dot + Spread', { name: 'Dot', color: '#ffffff', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 } }, { name: 'Lines', color: '#ff3b3b', lines: { gap: 5, length: 5 }, firing: FIRE(12, 0, 150) }),

    preset('Layered', 'Bullet Drop',
      { name: 'Main', color: '#00ff66', lines: { gap: 3, length: 6 } },
      { name: 'Drop 1', color: '#00ff66', offsetY: 14, lines: { top: false, bottom: false, length: 4, gap: 1, thickness: 1 } },
      { name: 'Drop 2', color: '#00ff66', offsetY: 26, lines: { top: false, bottom: false, length: 3, gap: 1, thickness: 1 } },
      { name: 'Drop 3', color: '#00ff66', offsetY: 38, lines: { top: false, bottom: false, length: 2, gap: 1, thickness: 1 } }),
    preset('Layered', 'Dot in Ring X',
      { name: 'X', color: '#ff2bd6', rotation: 45, lines: { length: 6, gap: 9 } },
      { name: 'Ring', color: '#ffffff', lines: NO_LINES, circle: { enabled: true, radius: 6 } },
      { name: 'Dot', color: '#ffffff', lines: NO_LINES, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 } }),
    preset('Layered', 'Double Cross',
      { name: 'Outer', color: '#00e5ff', lines: { gap: 10, length: 6, thickness: 1 } },
      { name: 'Inner', color: '#ffffff', lines: { gap: 2, length: 3, thickness: 1 } }),

    preset('Stylized', 'Big X', { color: '#ffe600', rotation: 45, lines: { length: 10, thickness: 2, gap: 5 } }),
    preset('Stylized', 'Diamond X', { color: '#00e5ff', rotation: 45, lines: { length: 4, thickness: 2, gap: 3 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 2 } }),
    preset('Stylized', 'Tactical', { color: '#c6ff3d', lines: { length: 12, thickness: 1, gap: 10 }, dot: { enabled: true, size: 3, shape: 'circle' }, outline: NO_OUTLINE }),
    preset('Stylized', 'Blocky', { color: '#ff8a00', lines: { length: 4, thickness: 4, gap: 4 }, outline: { thickness: 2 } }),
  ];

  return {
    LAYER_SCHEMA,
    DEFAULT_LAYER,
    DEFAULT_CROSSHAIR,
    MAX_LAYERS,
    MAX_IMAGE_CHARS,
    PRESETS,
    SHARE_PREFIX,
    sanitizeLayer,
    sanitizeCrosshair,
    crosshairExtent,
    overlaySizePx,
    drawCrosshair,
    layerBounds,
    onImageLoaded,
    FireAnimator,
    hasFiring,
    hasImageLayers,
    encodeShareCode,
    decodeShareCode,
  };
});

```

`src/shared/crosshairx-import.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/**
 * Converts crosshairs saved by Crosshair X (user's local JSON data files) into Crosshair Z format.
 * Works on the documented-by-example data shape only:
 *   { name?, pos:{x,y}, model:[{ type:'model', line, dot, outline, position, layer:{size}, firingOptions }] }
 * Unknown/unsupported parts (custom images, animations beyond a simple spread) are skipped.
 */
const core = require('./crosshair-core');

const ARMS = ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right'];
const DEFAULT_ANIM_MS = 120;

function num(v, fallback = 0) {
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : fallback;
}

function color(v, fallback) {
  return typeof v === 'string' && /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(v) ? v.toLowerCase() : fallback;
}

function obj(v) {
  return v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? v : {};
}

/** Firing: map simple outward/inward offset to spread; everything else is not representable. */
function convertFiring(fo) {
  const f = obj(fo);
  const off = num(f.firingOffset);
  if (!off) return { enabled: false };
  return { enabled: true, spread: Math.abs(Math.round(off)), kick: 0, speed: Math.max(20, num(f.duration, DEFAULT_ANIM_MS) || DEFAULT_ANIM_MS) };
}

/**
 * Convert one Crosshair X model entry into 1–2 Z layers.
 * Dot gets its own layer when its opacity differs from the lines (Z has one opacity per layer).
 * @returns {{layers: object[], warnings: string[]}}
 */
function convertModel(m, index, pos) {
  const warnings = [];
  const line = obj(m.line);
  const dot = obj(m.dot);
  const outline = obj(m.outline);
  const position = obj(m.position);
  const scale = num(obj(m.layer).size, 1) || 1;
  const visible = Array.isArray(line.visible) ? line.visible : ARMS;

  const lineOpacity = num(line.opacity, 1);
  const dotOpacity = num(dot.opacity, 0);
  const linesOn = lineOpacity > 0 && num(line.length) > 0 && visible.length > 0;
  const dotOn = dotOpacity > 0 && num(dot.diameter) > 0;
  const outlineOn = num(outline.thickness) > 0 && num(outline.opacity) > 0;
  if (line.shape && line.shape !== 'rectangle') warnings.push(`line shape "${line.shape}" drawn as rectangle`);
  if (m.type && m.type !== 'model') warnings.push(`layer type "${m.type}" not supported`);

  const base = {
    name: `Layer ${index + 1}`,
    visible: true,
    kind: 'shape',
    offsetX: Math.round(num(position.x) + num(pos.x)),
    offsetY: Math.round(num(position.y) + num(pos.y)),
    color: color(line.color, '#ffffff'),
    rotation: Math.round(num(line.rotation)),
    scale,
    blur: Math.round(Math.max(num(line.blur), num(dot.blur))),
    lines: {
      enabled: linesOn,
      length: Math.round(num(line.length)),
      thickness: Math.max(1, Math.round(num(line.thickness, 1))),
      gap: Math.round(num(line.offset)),
      top: visible.includes('top'),
      bottom: visible.includes('bottom'),
      left: visible.includes('left'),
      right: visible.includes('right'),
    },
    dot: {
      enabled: false,
      size: Math.max(1, Math.round(num(dot.diameter, 2))),
      shape: dot.shape === 'square' ? 'square' : 'circle',
      color: color(dot.color, '#ffffff'),
      useMainColor: false,
    },
    circle: { enabled: false },
    firing: convertFiring(m.firingOptions),
  };

  // Z composites outline under the layer opacity → divide so effective outline alpha matches.
  const outlineFor = (layerOpacity) => ({
    enabled: outlineOn,
    thickness: Math.max(1, Math.round(num(outline.thickness, 1))),
    color: color(outline.color, '#000000'),
    opacity: layerOpacity > 0 ? Math.min(1, num(outline.opacity, 1) / layerOpacity) : 1,
  });

  const layers = [];
  const sameOpacity = !linesOn || !dotOn || Math.abs(lineOpacity - dotOpacity) < 0.001;
  if (sameOpacity) {
    const op = linesOn ? lineOpacity : dotOn ? dotOpacity : 1;
    layers.push({ ...base, opacity: op, dot: { ...base.dot, enabled: dotOn }, outline: outlineFor(op) });
  } else {
    layers.push({ ...base, opacity: lineOpacity, outline: outlineFor(lineOpacity) });
    layers.push({
      ...base,
      name: `Layer ${index + 1} dot`,
      opacity: dotOpacity,
      lines: { ...base.lines, enabled: false },
      dot: { ...base.dot, enabled: true },
      outline: outlineFor(dotOpacity),
    });
  }
  if (!linesOn && !dotOn) warnings.push(`layer ${index + 1} is empty`);
  return { layers, warnings };
}

const COLOR_NAMES = [
  ['white', [255, 255, 255]], ['black', [0, 0, 0]], ['grey', [128, 128, 128]],
  ['red', [230, 40, 40]], ['orange', [255, 140, 0]], ['yellow', [250, 230, 30]],
  ['green', [40, 220, 60]], ['cyan', [0, 220, 230]], ['blue', [40, 80, 255]],
  ['purple', [140, 60, 220]], ['pink', [255, 60, 200]],
];

/** Nearest plain color word for a hex color. */
function colorName(hex) {
  const v = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  const rgb = [(v >> 16) & 255, (v >> 8) & 255, v & 255];
  let best = COLOR_NAMES[0];
  let bestD = Infinity;
  for (const entry of COLOR_NAMES) {
    const d = entry[1].reduce((acc, c, i) => acc + (c - rgb[i]) ** 2, 0);
    if (d < bestD) {
      bestD = d;
      best = entry;
    }
  }
  return best[0];
}

/** Short human name from the first visible layer, e.g. "Green cross", "Red dot", "White T". */
function describe(ch) {
  const l = ch.layers.find((x) => x.visible && (x.lines.enabled || x.dot.enabled || x.circle.enabled)) || ch.layers[0];
  const L = l.lines;
  const arms = ['top', 'bottom', 'left', 'right'].filter((a) => L[a]).length;
  let shape = 'dot';
  if (L.enabled && L.length > 0) {
    if (arms === 3) shape = 'T';
    else if (arms < 3) shape = 'lines';
    else shape = Math.abs(l.rotation % 90) === 45 ? 'X' : 'cross';
    if (l.dot.enabled) shape += ' with dot';
  } else if (l.circle.enabled) {
    shape = 'circle';
  }
  const color = colorName(l.lines.enabled ? l.color : l.dot.useMainColor ? l.color : l.dot.color);
  const name = `${color} ${shape}`;
  return name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + name.slice(1);
}

/**
 * Convert a parsed Crosshair X crosshair JSON.
 * @param {unknown} data
 * @param {string} fallbackName
 * @returns {{name: string, crosshair: object, warnings: string[]}}
 * @throws {Error} when data is not a Crosshair X crosshair
 */
function convertCrosshairX(data, fallbackName) {
  const src = obj(data);
  if (!Array.isArray(src.model) || src.model.length === 0) throw new Error('not a Crosshair X crosshair (no model)');
  const pos = obj(src.pos);
  const warnings = [];
  const layers = [];
  src.model.forEach((m, i) => {
    const r = convertModel(obj(m), i, pos);
    layers.push(...r.layers);
    warnings.push(...r.warnings);
  });
  if (layers.length > core.MAX_LAYERS) warnings.push(`only first ${core.MAX_LAYERS} layers kept`);
  const crosshair = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers });
  const named = typeof src.name === 'string' && src.name.trim();
  const name = named ? src.name.trim().slice(0, 40) : fallbackName || describe(crosshair);
  return { name, crosshair, warnings };
}

module.exports = { convertCrosshairX, describe };

```

`src/shared/default-pack.js`:

```js
'use strict';

/**
 * Starter pack shipped with the app (src/shared/default-pack.json, built by scripts/build-default-pack.js).
 * Entries are CZ2 share codes; invalid entries are skipped, never fatal.
 */
const core = require('./crosshair-core');

const PACK_CATEGORY = 'Starter pack';

/** @returns {{name: string, category: string, crosshair: object}[]} */
function loadDefaultPack() {
  let raw;
  try {
    raw = require('./default-pack.json');
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('default pack missing or unreadable:', err.message);
    return [];
  }
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return [];
  const out = [];
  for (const entry of raw) {
    try {
      out.push({ name: String(entry.name || 'Crosshair').slice(0, 40), category: PACK_CATEGORY, crosshair: core.decodeShareCode(entry.code) });
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('skipping invalid default pack entry:', entry && entry.name, err.message);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

module.exports = { loadDefaultPack, PACK_CATEGORY };
```

`test/core.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');
const core = require('../src/shared/crosshair-core');
const { sanitizeConfig, defaultConfig, dedupeProfiles, fingerprint } = require('../src/main/store');

const plain = (o) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));

test('sanitize fills defaults from garbage', () => {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(core.sanitizeCrosshair(null), plain(core.DEFAULT_CROSSHAIR));
  assert.deepStrictEqual(core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [] }), plain(core.DEFAULT_CROSSHAIR));
});

test('legacy flat crosshair becomes single layer', () => {
  const ch = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ color: '#ff0000', lines: { gap: 7 } });
  assert.strictEqual(ch.layers.length, 1);
  assert.strictEqual(ch.layers[0].color, '#ff0000');
  assert.strictEqual(ch.layers[0].lines.gap, 7);
});

test('sanitize clamps numbers, rejects bad colors/enums/images, caps layers', () => {
  const l = core.sanitizeLayer({
    opacity: 5, scale: -1, color: 'red', lines: { length: 1e9, thickness: 'abc', gap: 2.6 }, dot: { shape: 'star' },
    image: { src: 'javascript:alert(1)' }, offsetX: null,
  });
  assert.strictEqual(l.opacity, 1);
  assert.strictEqual(l.scale, 0.25);
  assert.strictEqual(l.color, core.DEFAULT_LAYER.color);
  assert.strictEqual(l.lines.length, 100);
  assert.strictEqual(l.lines.thickness, core.DEFAULT_LAYER.lines.thickness);
  assert.strictEqual(l.lines.gap, 3);
  assert.strictEqual(l.dot.shape, 'square');
  assert.strictEqual(l.image.src, '');
  assert.strictEqual(l.offsetX, 0);
  assert.strictEqual(core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: Array(20).fill({}) }).layers.length, core.MAX_LAYERS);
});

test('share code roundtrip for every preset', () => {
  for (const p of core.PRESETS) {
    const code = core.encodeShareCode(p.crosshair);
    assert.ok(code.startsWith(core.SHARE_PREFIX));
    assert.deepStrictEqual(core.decodeShareCode(code), p.crosshair, p.name);
  }
});

test('share code drops image layers, rejects image-only', () => {
  const img = { kind: 'image', image: { src: 'data:image/png;base64,AAAA', size: 32 } };
  const ch = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{}, img] });
  assert.strictEqual(core.decodeShareCode(core.encodeShareCode(ch)).layers.length, 1);
  assert.throws(() => core.encodeShareCode({ layers: [img] }), /cannot be shared/);
});

test('legacy CZ1 code still decodes', () => {
  const legacy = 'CZ1-' + Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ color: '#123456' })).toString('base64url');
  assert.strictEqual(core.decodeShareCode(legacy).layers[0].color, '#123456');
});

test('share code rejects invalid input', () => {
  assert.throws(() => core.decodeShareCode(''), /must start/);
  assert.throws(() => core.decodeShareCode('CZ2-!!'), /invalid characters/);
  assert.throws(() => core.decodeShareCode('CZ2-AAAA'), /corrupted/);
});

test('overlay size even, covers extent incl. firing + offsets + global scale', () => {
  for (const p of core.PRESETS) {
    for (const g of [0.5, 1, 2]) {
      const s = core.overlaySizePx(p.crosshair, g);
      assert.strictEqual(s % 2, 0);
      assert.ok(s / 2 >= core.crosshairExtent(p.crosshair, g));
    }
  }
  const base = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{}] });
  const fired = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{ firing: { enabled: true, spread: 30, kick: 20 } }] });
  const moved = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{ offsetY: 100 }] });
  assert.ok(core.crosshairExtent(fired) > core.crosshairExtent(base));
  assert.ok(core.crosshairExtent(moved) >= 100);
});

test('fire animator ramps up and down per layer speed', () => {
  const ch = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{ firing: { enabled: true, speed: 100 } }, {}] });
  const a = new core.FireAnimator();
  a.setFiring(true);
  assert.strictEqual(a.step(50, ch), true);
  assert.strictEqual(a.levels[0], 0.5);
  assert.strictEqual(a.levels[1], 0);
  a.step(60, ch);
  assert.strictEqual(a.levels[0], 1);
  a.setFiring(false);
  a.step(200, ch);
  assert.strictEqual(a.levels[0], 0);
});

test('config sanitize repairs broken config', () => {
  const cfg = sanitizeConfig({
    profiles: [{ id: 'a', name: '', hotkey: 5 }, { id: 'a' }, 5], activeProfileId: 'zzz',
    display: { offsetX: 1e9, scale: 99 }, toggle: { mode: 'x', profileId: 'nope' }, rightClick: { mode: 'hideHold', profileId: 'a' },
  });
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.profiles.length, 2);
  assert.notStrictEqual(cfg.profiles[0].id, cfg.profiles[1].id);
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.activeProfileId, cfg.profiles[0].id);
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.profiles[0].hotkey, '');
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.display.offsetX, 4000);
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.display.scale, 4);
  assert.deepStrictEqual(cfg.toggle, { mode: 'hide', profileId: null });
  assert.deepStrictEqual(cfg.rightClick, { mode: 'hideHold', profileId: 'a' });
  assert.ok(sanitizeConfig(null).profiles.length > 0);
  assert.strictEqual(defaultConfig().enabled, true);
});

test('v3 migration clears legacy optional hotkeys, keeps custom ones', () => {
  const cfg = sanitizeConfig({
    version: 2,
    hotkeys: { toggle: 'F8', next: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+Right', moveUp: 'F9', settings: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+S' },
  });
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.hotkeys.toggle, 'F8');
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.hotkeys.next, '');
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.hotkeys.settings, '');
  assert.strictEqual(cfg.hotkeys.moveUp, 'F9');
  assert.strictEqual(defaultConfig().hotkeys.next, '');
  // Once on v3, user may re-bind a legacy combo and it must stick.
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 3, hotkeys: { next: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+Right' } }).hotkeys.next, 'CommandOrControl+Shift+Right');
});

test('dedupeProfiles keeps one per design, prefers active/hotkey, keeps order', () => {
  const red = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{ color: '#ff0000' }] });
  const blue = core.sanitizeCrosshair({ layers: [{ color: '#0000ff' }] });
  const ps = [
    { id: 'a', name: 'Red', crosshair: red },
    { id: 'b', name: 'Blue', crosshair: blue },
    { id: 'c', name: 'Red copy', crosshair: red, hotkey: 'F1' },
    { id: 'd', name: 'Red again', crosshair: red },
    { id: 'e', name: 'Blue copy', crosshair: blue },
  ];
  assert.deepStrictEqual(dedupeProfiles(ps, 'e').map((p) => p.id), ['c', 'e']);
  assert.deepStrictEqual(dedupeProfiles(ps, 'zzz').map((p) => p.id), ['b', 'c']);
  assert.strictEqual(fingerprint({ layers: [{ color: '#FF0000' }] }), fingerprint(red));
});

test('starter pack decodes completely and new configs start from it', () => {
  const { loadDefaultPack } = require('../src/shared/default-pack');
  const raw = require('../src/shared/default-pack.json');
  const pack = loadDefaultPack();
  assert.ok(pack.length > 0);
  assert.strictEqual(pack.length, raw.length, 'every entry must decode');
  assert.deepStrictEqual(defaultConfig().profiles[0].crosshair, pack[0].crosshair);
});

test('toggle default is Alt+Shift+X; old untouched defaults migrate, custom keys stay', () => {
  assert.strictEqual(defaultConfig().hotkeys.toggle, 'Alt+Shift+X');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 3, hotkeys: { toggle: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+X' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'Alt+Shift+X');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 4, hotkeys: { toggle: 'Alt+Shift+Z' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'Alt+Shift+X');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 3, hotkeys: { toggle: 'Alt+Shift+Z' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'Alt+Shift+Z');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 3, hotkeys: { toggle: 'F7' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'F7');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 4, hotkeys: { toggle: 'CommandOrControl+Shift+X' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'CommandOrControl+Shift+X');
  assert.strictEqual(sanitizeConfig({ version: 5, hotkeys: { toggle: 'Alt+Shift+Z' } }).hotkeys.toggle, 'Alt+Shift+Z');
});

```

`test/crosshairx-import.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');
const { convertCrosshairX } = require('../src/shared/crosshairx-import');

/** Shape taken from a real Crosshair X saved crosshair file. */
const sample = (patch = {}) => ({
  name: 'My crosshair',
  pos: { x: 0, y: 0 },
  model: [{
    dot: { diameter: 4, opacity: 0, color: '#FFFFFF', shape: 'circle', blur: 0 },
    line: { length: 4, thickness: 2, offset: 2, opacity: 1, color: '#FFFFFF', shape: 'rectangle', blur: 0, rotation: 0, visible: ['left', 'right', 'top', 'bottom'] },
    outline: { thickness: 1, opacity: 0.8, color: '#0000FF', blur: 0 },
    firingOptions: { firingOffset: 0 },
    position: { x: 0, y: 0 },
    layer: { size: 1 },
    type: 'model',
    ...patch,
  }],
});

test('maps lines, outline, scale, name', () => {
  const r = convertCrosshairX(sample(), 'fallback');
  assert.strictEqual(r.name, 'My crosshair');
  assert.strictEqual(r.crosshair.layers.length, 1);
  const l = r.crosshair.layers[0];
  assert.deepStrictEqual([l.lines.length, l.lines.thickness, l.lines.gap], [4, 2, 2]);
  assert.strictEqual(l.color, '#ffffff');
  assert.strictEqual(l.dot.enabled, false);
  assert.deepStrictEqual([l.outline.enabled, l.outline.color, l.outline.opacity], [true, '#0000ff', 0.8]);
  assert.strictEqual(l.scale, 1);
});

test('outline thickness/opacity 0 disables outline; layer.size → scale', () => {
  const l = convertCrosshairX(sample({ outline: { thickness: 0, opacity: 0, color: '#FF0000' }, layer: { size: 1.18 } }), 'x').crosshair.layers[0];
  assert.strictEqual(l.outline.enabled, false);
  assert.strictEqual(l.scale, 1.18);
});

test('visible arms subset → T-shape', () => {
  const l = convertCrosshairX(sample({ line: { length: 5, thickness: 2, offset: 3, opacity: 1, color: '#00FF00', visible: ['left', 'right', 'bottom'] } }), 'x').crosshair.layers[0];
  assert.deepStrictEqual([l.lines.top, l.lines.bottom, l.lines.left, l.lines.right], [false, true, true, true]);
});

test('dot with different opacity becomes separate layer, keeps dot color', () => {
  const r = convertCrosshairX(sample({ dot: { diameter: 3, opacity: 0.5, color: '#FF0000', shape: 'square' } }), 'x');
  assert.strictEqual(r.crosshair.layers.length, 2);
  const [lines, dot] = r.crosshair.layers;
  assert.strictEqual(lines.dot.enabled, false);
  assert.deepStrictEqual([dot.lines.enabled, dot.dot.enabled, dot.dot.size, dot.dot.shape, dot.dot.color, dot.opacity], [false, true, 3, 'square', '#ff0000', 0.5]);
});

test('dot with same opacity stays in one layer', () => {
  const r = convertCrosshairX(sample({ dot: { diameter: 2, opacity: 1, color: '#00FF00', shape: 'circle' } }), 'x');
  assert.strictEqual(r.crosshair.layers.length, 1);
  assert.strictEqual(r.crosshair.layers[0].dot.enabled, true);
  assert.strictEqual(r.crosshair.layers[0].dot.useMainColor, false);
});

test('position + global pos → offsets; firing offset → spread', () => {
  const data = sample({ position: { x: 3, y: -4 }, firingOptions: { firingOffset: 5, duration: 200 } });
  data.pos = { x: 1, y: 1 };
  const l = convertCrosshairX(data, 'x').crosshair.layers[0];
  assert.deepStrictEqual([l.offsetX, l.offsetY], [4, -3]);
  assert.deepStrictEqual([l.firing.enabled, l.firing.spread, l.firing.speed], [true, 5, 200]);
});

test('missing name uses fallback; garbage rejected', () => {
  const d = sample();
  delete d.name;
  assert.strictEqual(convertCrosshairX(d, 'CrosshairX 123').name, 'CrosshairX 123');
  assert.throws(() => convertCrosshairX({}, 'x'), /not a Crosshair X/);
  assert.throws(() => convertCrosshairX(null, 'x'), /not a Crosshair X/);
});

test('unnamed crosshairs get a descriptive name', () => {
  const d = sample();
  delete d.name;
  assert.strictEqual(convertCrosshairX(d).name, 'White cross');
  const dot = sample({ line: { length: 0, opacity: 0 }, dot: { diameter: 3, opacity: 1, color: '#FF0000' } });
  delete dot.name;
  assert.strictEqual(convertCrosshairX(dot).name, 'Red dot');
  const t = sample({ line: { length: 5, thickness: 2, offset: 2, opacity: 1, color: '#00FF00', visible: ['left', 'right', 'bottom'] } });
  delete t.name;
  assert.strictEqual(convertCrosshairX(t).name, 'Green T');
});

```

`test/updater.test.js`:

```js
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');
const fs = require('fs');
const os = require('os');
const path = require('path');
const { compareVersions, parseRelease, checkForUpdate, downloadUpdate, ASSET_NAME } = require('../src/main/updater');

const silentLog = { info() {}, warn() {}, error() {} };

const release = (tag, size = 5) => ({
  tag_name: tag,
  html_url: 'https://github.com/Nn1ff/crosshair-z/releases/tag/' + tag,
  assets: [{ name: ASSET_NAME, size, browser_download_url: 'https://github.com/Nn1ff/crosshair-z/releases/download/' + tag + '/' + ASSET_NAME }],
});
const jsonRes = (body, status = 200) => ({ ok: status >= 200 && status < 300, status, json: async () => body });

test('compareVersions handles multi-digit parts', () => {
  assert.ok(compareVersions('1.7.10', '1.7.9') > 0);
  assert.ok(compareVersions('1.8.0', '1.7.11') > 0);
  assert.strictEqual(compareVersions('1.7.11', '1.7.11'), 0);
  assert.ok(compareVersions('1.7.2', '1.7.10') < 0);
});

test('parseRelease accepts a valid payload and rejects bad ones', () => {
  assert.deepStrictEqual(parseRelease(release('v1.7.12', 42)), {
    version: '1.7.12',
    url: 'https://github.com/Nn1ff/crosshair-z/releases/download/v1.7.12/Crosshair-Z.exe',
    size: 42,
    page: 'https://github.com/Nn1ff/crosshair-z/releases/tag/v1.7.12',
  });
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease(null), null);
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease({ ...release('v1.7.12'), prerelease: true }), null);
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease(release('latest')), null);
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease({ ...release('v1.7.12'), assets: [] }), null);
  const evil = release('v1.7.12');
  evil.assets[0].browser_download_url = 'https://evil.example/x.exe';
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease(evil), null);
  assert.strictEqual(parseRelease(release('v1.7.12', 0)), null);
});

test('checkForUpdate returns newer release, null when same/older', async () => {
  const newer = await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => jsonRes(release('v1.7.12')), currentVersion: '1.7.11', log: silentLog });
  assert.strictEqual(newer.version, '1.7.12');
  assert.strictEqual(await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => jsonRes(release('v1.7.11')), currentVersion: '1.7.11', log: silentLog }), null);
  assert.strictEqual(await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => jsonRes(release('v1.7.10')), currentVersion: '1.7.11', log: silentLog }), null);
});

test('checkForUpdate never throws: offline retries then gives up, 403 does not retry', async () => {
  let calls = 0;
  const offline = async () => { calls++; throw new TypeError('fetch failed'); };
  assert.strictEqual(await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: offline, currentVersion: '1.0.0', log: silentLog, retryBaseMs: 1 }), null);
  assert.strictEqual(calls, 3);
  calls = 0;
  const limited = async () => { calls++; return jsonRes({}, 403); };
  assert.strictEqual(await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: limited, currentVersion: '1.0.0', log: silentLog, retryBaseMs: 1 }), null);
  assert.strictEqual(calls, 1);
  assert.strictEqual(await checkForUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => jsonRes({}, 404), currentVersion: '1.0.0', log: silentLog }), null);
});

test('downloadUpdate writes the file and rejects size mismatch without leftovers', async () => {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'cz-upd-'));
  try {
    const bodyRes = (text) => ({ ok: true, status: 200, body: new Blob([text]).stream() });
    const rel = parseRelease(release('v1.7.12', 5));
    const dest = await downloadUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => bodyRes('hello'), release: rel, dir, log: silentLog });
    assert.strictEqual(path.basename(dest), 'Crosshair Z 1.7.12.exe');
    assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(dest, 'utf8'), 'hello');

    const bad = parseRelease(release('v1.7.13', 99));
    await assert.rejects(downloadUpdate({ fetchFn: async () => bodyRes('short'), release: bad, dir, log: silentLog }), /size mismatch/);
    assert.deepStrictEqual(fs.readdirSync(dir).sort(), ['Crosshair Z 1.7.12.exe']);
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});

```
